+        metadata: { task_id: 'task-1' },

+
+    const response = await completeTaskHandler(

+    expect(response.status).toBe(500);

+      recoverable: false,

import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
import { uploadImageToStorage } from '@/shared/lib/media/imageUploader';
import { createImageInpaintTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageEditing/imageInpaint';
import { createMaskedEditTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageEditing/maskedEditTaskBuilder';
import type { MaskedEditTaskParams } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageEditing/maskedEditTaskBuilder';
import { buildMaskedEditTaskParams } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageEditing/buildMaskedEditTaskParams';
import { convertToHiresFixApiParams } from '../useGenerationEditSettings';
import { getMediaUrl } from '@/shared/lib/media/mediaTypeHelpers';
import type { StrokeOverlayHandle } from '../../components/StrokeOverlay';
import type { EditAdvancedSettings, QwenEditModel } from './types';

type TaskType = 'inpaint' | 'annotate';

interface TaskTypeConfig {
  fileNamePrefix: string;
  createTask: typeof createImageInpaintTask | typeof createAnnotatedImageEditTask;
}

const TASK_CONFIGS: Record<TaskType, TaskTypeConfig> = {
  inpaint: {
    fileNamePrefix: 'inpaint_mask',
    createTask: createImageInpaintTask,
  },
  annotate: {
    fileNamePrefix: 'annotated_edit_mask',
    createTask: createAnnotatedImageEditTask,
  },
};

interface CreateInpaintingTaskWorkflowParams {
  taskType: TaskType;
  media: GenerationRow;
  selectedProjectId: string;
  shotId?: string;
  toolTypeOverride?: string;
  loras?: Array<{ url: string; strength: number }>;
  activeVariantId?: string | null;
  activeVariantLocation?: string | null;
  createAsGeneration?: boolean;
  advancedSettings?: EditAdvancedSettings;
  qwenEditModel?: QwenEditModel;
  inpaintPrompt: string;
  inpaintNumGenerations: number;
  actualGenerationId: string;
  strokeOverlay: StrokeOverlayHandle;
}

function createAnnotatedImageEditTask(params: MaskedEditTaskParams): Promise<string> {
  return createMaskedEditTask({
    taskType: 'annotated_image_edit',
    context: 'createAnnotatedImageEditTask',
    batchOperationName: 'AnnotatedImageEdit',
  }, params);
}

export async function createInpaintingTaskWorkflow({
  taskType,
  media,
  selectedProjectId,
  shotId,
  toolTypeOverride,
  loras,
  activeVariantId,
  activeVariantLocation,
  createAsGeneration,
  advancedSettings,
  qwenEditModel,
  inpaintPrompt,
  inpaintNumGenerations,
  actualGenerationId,
  strokeOverlay,
}: CreateInpaintingTaskWorkflowParams): Promise<string> {
  const config = TASK_CONFIGS[taskType];
  const maskImageData = strokeOverlay.exportMask({ pixelRatio: 1.5 });

  if (!maskImageData) {
    throw new Error('Failed to export mask from overlay');
  }

  const maskBlob = await fetch(maskImageData).then((res) => res.blob());
  const maskFile = new File(
    [maskBlob],
    `${config.fileNamePrefix}_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`,
    { type: 'image/png' },
  );
  const maskUrl = await uploadImageToStorage(maskFile);

  const mediaUrl = getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl;
  const sourceUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
  if (!sourceUrl) {
    throw new Error('Missing source media URL');
  }

  return config.createTask(
    buildMaskedEditTaskParams({
      projectId: selectedProjectId,
      imageUrl: sourceUrl,
      maskUrl,
      prompt: inpaintPrompt,
      numGenerations: inpaintNumGenerations,
      generationId: actualGenerationId,
      shotId,
      toolType: toolTypeOverride,
      loras,
      createAsGeneration,
      sourceVariantId: activeVariantId || undefined,
      hiresFix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings),
      qwenEditModel,
    }),
  );
}
```

@@ -1,6 +1,62 @@
 import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
 import { ExpectedSegmentData, RawGenerationDbRow } from './segmentOutputTypes';
 
+function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
+  return value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
+    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
+    : null;
+}
+
+function asNullableString(value: unknown): string | null | undefined {
+  if (value === null) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined;
+}
+
+function asNullableNumber(value: unknown): number | null | undefined {
+  if (value === null) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : undefined;
+}
+
+export function coerceRawGenerationDbRow(value: unknown): RawGenerationDbRow | null {
+  const record = asRecord(value);
+  if (!record || typeof record.id !== 'string') {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  return {
+    id: record.id,
+    ...(asNullableString(record.location) !== undefined ? { location: asNullableString(record.location) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.thumbnail_url) !== undefined ? { thumbnail_url: asNullableString(record.thumbnail_url) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.type) !== undefined ? { type: asNullableString(record.type) } : {}),
+    ...(typeof record.created_at === 'string' ? { created_at: record.created_at } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.updated_at) !== undefined ? { updated_at: asNullableString(record.updated_at) } : {}),
+    ...(asRecord(record.params) || record.params === null ? { params: (asRecord(record.params) ?? null) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.parent_generation_id) !== undefined ? { parent_generation_id: asNullableString(record.parent_generation_id) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableNumber(record.child_order) !== undefined ? { child_order: asNullableNumber(record.child_order) } : {}),
+    ...(typeof record.starred === 'boolean' ? { starred: record.starred } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.pair_shot_generation_id) !== undefined ? { pair_shot_generation_id: asNullableString(record.pair_shot_generation_id) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.primary_variant_id) !== undefined ? { primary_variant_id: asNullableString(record.primary_variant_id) } : {}),
+  };
+}
+
+export function coerceRawGenerationDbRows(value: unknown): RawGenerationDbRow[] {
+  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
+    return [];
+  }
+
+  return value.map((entry, index) => {
+    const row = coerceRawGenerationDbRow(entry);
+    if (!row) {
+      throw new Error(`Invalid generation row at index ${index}`);
+    }
+    return row;
+  });
+}
+

 ): ExpectedSegmentData | null {

+function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
+  return value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
+    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
+    : null;
+}
+
+function asNullableString(value: unknown): string | null | undefined {
+  if (value === null) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined;
+}
+
+function asNullableNumber(value: unknown): number | null | undefined {
+  if (value === null) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : undefined;
+}
+
+function asJsonRecord(value: unknown): Json | null | undefined {
+  if (value === null) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return asRecord(value) as Json | null;
+}
+
+export function coerceGenerationRowDto(
+  value: unknown,
+): (GenerationRowDto & { params?: Json | null }) | null {
+  const record = asRecord(value);
+  if (!record || typeof record.id !== 'string') {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  return {
+    id: record.id,
+    ...(typeof record.generation_id === 'string' ? { generation_id: record.generation_id } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.location) !== undefined ? { location: asNullableString(record.location) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.thumbnail_url) !== undefined ? { thumbnail_url: asNullableString(record.thumbnail_url) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.type) !== undefined ? { type: asNullableString(record.type) } : {}),
+    ...(typeof record.createdAt === 'string' ? { createdAt: record.createdAt } : {}),
+    ...(typeof record.created_at === 'string' ? { created_at: record.created_at } : {}),
+    ...(asJsonRecord(record.metadata) !== undefined ? { metadata: asJsonRecord(record.metadata) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.name) !== undefined ? { name: asNullableString(record.name) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableNumber(record.timeline_frame) !== undefined ? { timeline_frame: asNullableNumber(record.timeline_frame) } : {}),
+    ...(typeof record.starred === 'boolean' ? { starred: record.starred } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.based_on) !== undefined ? { based_on: asNullableString(record.based_on) } : {}),
+    ...(asJsonRecord(record.params) !== undefined ? { params: asJsonRecord(record.params) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.parent_generation_id) !== undefined ? { parent_generation_id: asNullableString(record.parent_generation_id) } : {}),
+    ...(typeof record.is_child === 'boolean' ? { is_child: record.is_child } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableNumber(record.child_order) !== undefined ? { child_order: asNullableNumber(record.child_order) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.pair_shot_generation_id) !== undefined ? { pair_shot_generation_id: asNullableString(record.pair_shot_generation_id) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.primary_variant_id) !== undefined ? { primary_variant_id: asNullableString(record.primary_variant_id) } : {}),
+    ...(asNullableString(record.source_task_id) !== undefined ? { source_task_id: asNullableString(record.source_task_id) } : {}),
+  } satisfies GenerationRowDto & { params?: Json | null };
+}

1→import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     2→import { ExpectedSegmentData, RawGenerationDbRow } from './segmentOutputTypes';
     3→
     4→function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
     5→  return value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
     6→    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
     7→    : null;
     8→}
     9→
    10→function asNullableString(value: unknown): string | null | undefined {
    11→  if (value === null) {
    12→    return null;
    13→  }
    14→  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined;
    15→}
    16→
    17→function asNullableNumber(value: unknown): number | null | undefined {
    18→  if (value === null) {
    19→    return null;
    20→  }
    21→  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : undefined;
    22→}
    23→
    24→export function coerceRawGenerationDbRow(value: unknown): RawGenerationDbRow | null {
    25→  const record = asRecord(value);
    26→  if (!record || typeof record.id !== 'string') {
    27→    return null;
    28→  }
    29→
    30→  return {
    31→    id: record.id,
    32→    ...(asNullableString(record.location) !== undefined ? { location: asNullableString(record.location) } : {}),
    33→    ...(asNullableString(record.thumbnail_url) !== undefined ? { thumbnail_url: asNullableString(record.thumbnail_url) } : {}),
    34→    ...(asNullableString(record.type) !== undefined ? { type: asNullableString(record.type) } : {}),
    35→    ...(typeof record.created_at === 'string' ? { created_at: record.created_at } : {}),
    36→    ...(asNullableString(record.updated_at) !== undefined ? { updated_at: asNullableString(record.updated_at) } : {}),
    37→    ...(asRecord(record.params) || record.params === null ? { params: (asRecord(record.params) ?? null) } : {}),
    38→    ...(asNullableString(record.parent_generation_id) !== undefined ? { parent_generation_id: asNullableString(record.parent_generation_id) } : {}),
    39→    ...(asNullableNumber(record.child_order) !== undefined ? { child_order: asNullableNumber(record.child_order) } : {}),
    40→    ...(typeof record.starred === 'boolean' ? { starred: record.starred } : {}),
    41→    ...(asNullableString(record.pair_shot_generation_id) !== undefined ? { pair_shot_generation_id: asNullableString(record.pair_shot_generation_id) } : {}),
    42→    ...(asNullableString(record.primary_variant_id) !== undefined ? { primary_variant_id: asNullableString(record.primary_variant_id) } : {}),
    43→  };
    44→}
    45→
    46→export function coerceRawGenerationDbRows(value: unknown): RawGenerationDbRow[] {
    47→  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
    48→    return [];
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  return value.map((entry, index) => {
    52→    const row = coerceRawGenerationDbRow(entry);
    53→    if (!row) {
    54→      throw new Error(`Invalid generation row at index ${index}`);
    55→    }
    56→    return row;
    57→  });
    58→}
    59→
    60→export function extractExpectedSegmentData(
    61→  parentParams: Record<string, unknown> | null,
    62→): ExpectedSegmentData | null {
    63→  if (!parentParams) {
    64→    return null;
    65→  }
    66→
    67→  const orchestratorDetails = parentParams.orchestrator_details as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    68→  if (!orchestratorDetails) {
    69→    return null;
    70→  }
    71→
    72→  const segmentCount = (orchestratorDetails.num_new_segments_to_generate as number)
    73→    || (orchestratorDetails.segment_frames_expanded as unknown[] | undefined)?.length
    74→    || 0;
    75→
    76→  if (segmentCount === 0) {
    77→    return null;
    78→  }
    79→
    80→  return {
    81→    count: segmentCount,
    82→    frames: (orchestratorDetails.segment_frames_expanded as number[]) || [],
    83→    prompts: (orchestratorDetails.enhanced_prompts_expanded as string[])
    84→      || (orchestratorDetails.base_prompts_expanded as string[])
    85→      || [],
    86→    inputImages: (orchestratorDetails.input_image_paths_resolved as string[]) || [],
    87→    inputImageGenIds: (orchestratorDetails.input_image_generation_ids as string[]) || [],
    88→    pairShotGenIds: (orchestratorDetails.pair_shot_generation_ids as string[]) || [],
    89→  };
    90→}
    91→
    92→export function getPairIdentifiers(
    93→  generation: { pair_shot_generation_id?: string | null } | null,
    94→  params: Record<string, unknown> | null,
    95→): { pairShotGenId?: string; startGenId?: string } {
    96→  const columnValue = generation?.pair_shot_generation_id;
    97→  if (!columnValue) {
    98→    return {};
    99→  }
   100→
   101→  const individualParams = params?.individual_segment_params as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   102→  return {
   103→    pairShotGenId: columnValue,
   104→    startGenId: (individualParams?.start_image_generation_id || params?.start_image_generation_id) as string | undefined,
   105→  };
   106→}
   107→
   108→export function isSegmentGeneration(params: Record<string, unknown> | null): boolean {
   109→  return typeof params?.segment_index === 'number';
   110→}
   111→
   112→export function transformToGenerationRow(gen: RawGenerationDbRow): GenerationRow {
   113→  const createdAt = gen.updated_at || gen.created_at || new Date().toISOString();
   114→  const location = gen.location || '';
   115→
   116→  return {
   117→    id: gen.id,
   118→    location,
   119→    imageUrl: location,
   120→    thumbUrl: gen.thumbnail_url || location,
   121→    type: gen.type || 'video',
   122→    created_at: createdAt,
   123→    createdAt,
   124→    params: gen.params ?? undefined,
   125→    parent_generation_id: gen.parent_generation_id,
   126→    child_order: gen.child_order,
   127→    starred: gen.starred,
   128→    pair_shot_generation_id: gen.pair_shot_generation_id,
   129→  };
   130→}

+
+function useAspectRatioLayout({
+  projectAspectRatio,

+  const defaultItemsPerPage = aspectRatioLayout.itemsPerPage;
+
+  const rawItemsPerPage = itemsPerPage ?? defaultItemsPerPage;
+  const actualItemsPerPage =

+    [effectiveColumnsPerRow, aspectRatioLayout.gridColumnClasses],
+  );
+
+  return {
+    aspectRatioLayout,
+    effectiveColumnsPerRow,
+    actualItemsPerPage,
+    gridColumnClasses,
+  };

+  const prevFilteredImagesRef = useRef<string>('');

+    const currentSignature = `${filtersHook.filteredImages.length}-${filtersHook.filteredImages[0]?.id?.substring(0,8) ?? 'none'}-${filtersHook.filteredImages[filtersHook.filteredImages.length-1]?.id?.substring(0,8) ?? 'none'}`;
+    prevFilteredImagesRef.current = currentSignature;
+    if (stateHook.state.pendingLightboxTarget && filtersHook.filteredImages.length > 0) {
+      const targetIndex = stateHook.state.pendingLightboxTarget === 'first' ? 0 : filtersHook.filteredImages.length - 1;
+      const targetImage = filtersHook.filteredImages[targetIndex];
+      if (targetImage) {
+        actionsHook.handleOpenLightbox(targetImage);
+        stateHook.setPendingLightboxTarget(null);
+        pendingTargetSetTimeRef.current = null;
+      }
+    }
+  }, [actionsHook, filtersHook.filteredImages, pendingTargetSetTimeRef, serverPage, stateHook]);
+  const {
+    handleNavigateToShot,
+    handleVisitShotFromNotifier,
+    handleSwitchToAssociatedShot,
+    handleShowAllShots,
+    handleShowTaskDetails,
+    itemShotWorkflow,
+    itemMobileInteraction,
+    itemFeatures,
+    itemActions,
+    itemLoading,
+    lightboxDeletingId,
+  } = useMediaGalleryViewInteractions({
+    allShots,
+    simplifiedShotOptions,
+    navigateToShot,
+    actionsHook,
+    formAssociatedShotId,
+    onSwitchToAssociatedShot,
+    filtersHook,
+    stateHook,
+    isMobile,
+    onCreateShot,
+    onAddToLastShot,
+    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,
+    showDelete,
+    showDownload,
+    showShare,
+    showEdit,
+    showStar,
+    showAddToShot,
+    enableSingleClick,
+    videosAsThumbnails,
+    onToggleStar,
+    onApplySettings,
+    onImageClick,
+    isDeleting,
+    currentViewingShotId,
+    activeLightboxMediaId: stateHook.state.activeLightboxMedia?.id,
+    downloadingImageId: stateHook.state.downloadingImageId,
+  });
+  const lightboxSession = useMediaGalleryLightboxSession({
+    stateHook,
+    actionsHook,
+    filtersHook,
+    paginationHook,
+    serverPage,
+    handleNextImage,
+    handlePreviousImage,
+    handleSetActiveLightboxIndex,
+    lightboxDeletingId,
+    onApplySettings,
+    simplifiedShotOptions,
+    onAddToLastShot,
+    onAddToLastShotWithoutPosition,
+    isMobile,
+    task,
+    taskDetailsLoading: taskDetailsData?.isLoading ?? false,
+    taskError,
+    inputImages,
+    lightboxTaskMapping,
+    onCreateShot,
+    handleNavigateToShot,
+    handleShowTaskDetails,
+    currentToolType,
+    showDelete,
+  });
+  return (
+    <TooltipProvider>
+      <div
+        ref={galleryContainerRef as React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>}
+        className={cn(
+          'space-y-6',
+          isMobile && !hidePagination ? 'pb-16' : (reducedSpacing ? 'pb-0' : ((!hidePagination && !hideBottomPagination) ? 'pb-[62px]' : 'pb-0')),
+          className
+        )}
+      >
+        {/* Header section with pagination and filters */}
+        <div ref={stateHook.galleryTopRef}>
+          <MediaGalleryHeader
+            totalPages={paginationHook.totalPages}
+            page={paginationHook.page}
+            isServerPagination={paginationHook.isServerPagination}
+            serverPage={serverPage}
+            rangeStart={paginationHook.rangeStart}
+            rangeEnd={paginationHook.rangeEnd}
+            totalFilteredItems={paginationHook.totalFilteredItems}
+            loadingButton={paginationHook.loadingButton}
+            darkSurface={darkSurface}
+            reducedSpacing={reducedSpacing}
+            hidePagination={hidePagination}
+            onPageChange={paginationHook.handlePageChange}
+            isPhoneOnly={isPhoneOnly}
+            hideTopFilters={hideTopFilters}
+            hideMediaTypeFilter={hideMediaTypeFilter}
+            showStarredOnly={filtersHook.showStarredOnly}
+            onStarredFilterChange={(val) => withPaginationReset(() => filtersHook.setShowStarredOnly(Boolean(val)))}
+            showShotFilter={showShotFilter}
+            allShots={simplifiedShotOptions}
+            shotFilter={filtersHook.shotFilter}
+            onShotFilterChange={(shotId) => withPaginationReset(() => filtersHook.setShotFilter(shotId))}
+            excludePositioned={filtersHook.excludePositioned}
+            onExcludePositionedChange={(v) => withPaginationReset(() => filtersHook.setExcludePositioned(v))}
+            showSearch={showSearch}
+            isSearchOpen={filtersHook.isSearchOpen}
+            searchTerm={filtersHook.searchTerm}
+            searchInputRef={filtersHook.searchInputRef}
+            toggleSearch={filtersHook.toggleSearch}
+            clearSearch={filtersHook.clearSearch}
+            handleSearchChange={(value) => withPaginationReset(() => filtersHook.setSearchTerm(value))}
+            mediaTypeFilter={filtersHook.mediaTypeFilter}
+            onMediaTypeFilterChange={(value) => withPaginationReset(() => filtersHook.setMediaTypeFilter(value))}
+          />
+        </div>
+        {/* Shot Filter Notifier */}
+        {!hideShotNotifier && (
+          <ShotNotifier
+            formAssociatedShotId={formAssociatedShotId}
+            shotFilter={filtersHook.shotFilter}
+            showShotFilter={showShotFilter}
+            allShots={simplifiedShotOptions}
+            onSwitchToAssociatedShot={handleSwitchToAssociatedShot}
+            onShowAllShots={handleShowAllShots}
+            onVisitShot={handleVisitShotFromNotifier}
+          />
+        )}
+        {/* Main Gallery Grid */}
+        <MediaGalleryGrid
+          images={images}
+          paginatedImages={paginatedImagesWithBadges}
+          filteredImages={filtersHook.filteredImages}
+          reducedSpacing={reducedSpacing}
+          darkSurface={darkSurface}
+          gridColumnClasses={gridColumnClasses}
+          columnsPerRow={effectiveColumnsPerRow}
+          projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
+          isGalleryLoading={paginationHook.isGalleryLoading}
+          isServerPagination={paginationHook.isServerPagination}
+          clearNavigation={paginationHook.clearNavigation}
+          effectivePage={effectivePage}
+          isMobile={isMobile}
+          isLightboxOpen={!!stateHook.state.activeLightboxMedia}
+          enableAdjacentPagePreloading={enableAdjacentPagePreloading}
+          page={paginationHook.page}
+          serverPage={serverPage}
+          totalFilteredItems={paginationHook.totalFilteredItems}
+          itemsPerPage={actualItemsPerPage}
+          selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId ?? undefined}
+          generationFilters={generationFilters}
+          hasFilters={hasFilters}
+          isBackfillLoading={stateHook.state.isBackfillLoading}
+          setIsBackfillLoading={stateHook.setIsBackfillLoading}
+          totalCount={totalCount}
+          offset={offset}
+          optimisticDeletedCount={stateHook.state.optimisticDeletedIds.size}
+          itemShotWorkflow={itemShotWorkflow}
+          itemMobileInteraction={itemMobileInteraction}
+          itemFeatures={itemFeatures}
+          itemActions={itemActions}
+          itemLoading={itemLoading}
+          hideBottomPagination={hideBottomPagination}
+        />
+      </div>
+      {/* Lightbox and Task Details */}
+      <MediaGalleryLightbox session={lightboxSession} />
+      {/* Mobile floating bottom bar with pagination and star filter - phones only, not iPad */}
+      {isPhoneOnly && !hidePagination && !stateHook.state.activeLightboxMedia && paginationHook.totalPages > 1 && (
+        <MobileBottomBar
+          isServerPagination={paginationHook.isServerPagination}
+          serverPage={serverPage}
+          page={paginationHook.page}
+          totalPages={paginationHook.totalPages}
+          loadingButton={paginationHook.loadingButton}
+          onPageChange={paginationHook.handlePageChange}
+          hideTopFilters={hideTopFilters}
+          showStarredOnly={filtersHook.showStarredOnly}
+          onToggleStarred={() => withPaginationReset(() => filtersHook.setShowStarredOnly(!filtersHook.showStarredOnly))}
+        />
+      )}
+    </TooltipProvider>
+  );
+});
+MediaGallery.displayName = 'MediaGallery';
+export { MediaGallery };

1→import { getSupabaseClient } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     2→import {
     3→  enqueueSettingsWrite,
     4→  initializeSettingsWriteQueue,
     5→  type QueuedWrite,
     6→} from '@/shared/lib/settingsWriteQueue';
     7→import { deepMerge } from '@/shared/lib/utils/deepEqual';
     8→import { isCancellationError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
     9→import { ToolSettingsError } from '@/shared/lib/toolSettingsService';
    10→
    11→export type SettingsScope = 'user' | 'project' | 'shot';
    12→type SettingsScopeTableName = 'users' | 'projects' | 'shots';
    13→
    14→const SETTINGS_SCOPE_TABLES: Record<SettingsScope, SettingsScopeTableName> = {
    15→  user: 'users',
    16→  project: 'projects',
    17→  shot: 'shots',
    18→};
    19→
    20→function assertNeverScope(scope: never): never {
    21→  throw new Error(`Unsupported settings scope: ${String(scope)}`);
    22→}
    23→
    24→function resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope: SettingsScope): SettingsScopeTableName {
    25→  switch (scope) {
    26→    case 'user':
    27→    case 'project':
    28→    case 'shot':
    29→      return SETTINGS_SCOPE_TABLES[scope];
    30→    default:
    31→      return assertNeverScope(scope);
    32→  }
    33→}
    34→
    35→function selectSettingsForScope(scope: SettingsScope, id: string) {
    36→  const tableName = resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope);
    37→  return getSupabaseClient()
    38→    .from(tableName)
    39→    .select('settings')
    40→    .eq('id', id)
    41→    .single();
    42→}
    43→
    44→function callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc(
    45→  tableName: SettingsScopeTableName,
    46→  id: string,
    47→  toolId: string,
    48→  settings: Record<string, unknown>,
    49→) {
    50→  return getSupabaseClient().rpc('update_tool_settings_atomic', {
    51→    p_table_name: tableName,
    52→    p_id: id,
    53→    p_tool_id: toolId,
    54→    p_settings: settings,
    55→  });
    56→}
    57→
    58→type SettingsWriteMode = 'debounced' | 'immediate';
    59→
    60→interface UpdateToolSettingsParams {
    61→  scope: SettingsScope;
    62→  id: string;
    63→  toolId: string;
    64→  patch: unknown;
    65→}
    66→
    67→interface UpdateToolSettingsOptions {
    68→  signal?: AbortSignal;
    69→  mode?: SettingsWriteMode;
    70→}
    71→
    72→interface AbortSignalCapable<T> {
    73→  abortSignal?: (signal: AbortSignal) => T;
    74→}
    75→
    76→function isAbortSignal(value: unknown): value is AbortSignal {
    77→  return !!value
    78→    && typeof value === 'object'
    79→    && 'aborted' in value
    80→    && typeof (value as AbortSignal).addEventListener === 'function';
    81→}
    82→
    83→function isSettingsWriteMode(value: unknown): value is SettingsWriteMode {
    84→  return value === 'debounced' || value === 'immediate';
    85→}
    86→
    87→function maybeAttachAbortSignal<T>(query: T, signal?: AbortSignal): T {
    88→  if (!signal) return query;
    89→
    90→  const candidate = query as T & AbortSignalCapable<T>;
    91→  if (typeof candidate.abortSignal === 'function') {
    92→    return candidate.abortSignal(signal);
    93→  }
    94→
    95→  return query;
    96→}
    97→
    98→function throwIfAborted(signal?: AbortSignal): void {
    99→  if (signal?.aborted) {
   100→    throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   101→      recoverable: true,
   102→      cause: signal.reason,
   103→    });
   104→  }
   105→}
   106→
   107→async function fetchSettingsForScope(
   108→  scope: SettingsScope,
   109→  id: string,
   110→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   111→) {
   112→  throwIfAborted(signal);
   113→
   114→  return maybeAttachAbortSignal(selectSettingsForScope(scope, id), signal);
   115→}
   116→
   117→async function rawUpdateToolSettings(write: QueuedWrite): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
   118→  const { scope, entityId: id, toolId, patch, signal } = write;
   119→
   120→  try {
   121→    if (scope !== 'user' && scope !== 'project' && scope !== 'shot') {
   122→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
   123→        'invalid_scope_identifier',
   124→        `Invalid scope: ${scope}`,
   125→      );
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    const tableName = resolveSettingsScopeTable(scope);
   129→    const { data: currentEntity, error: fetchError } = await fetchSettingsForScope(scope, id, signal);
   130→
   131→    if (fetchError) {
   132→      const errorMessage = fetchError.message || '';
   133→      if (
   134→        errorMessage.includes('ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES')
   135→        || errorMessage.includes('Failed to fetch')
   136→        || fetchError.code === 'ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES'
   137→      ) {
   138→        throw new ToolSettingsError(
   139→          'network',
   140→          `Network exhaustion: ${errorMessage}`,
   141→          { recoverable: true, cause: fetchError },
   142→        );
   143→      }
   144→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
   145→        'scope_fetch_failed',
   146→        `Failed to fetch current ${scope} settings: ${errorMessage}`,
   147→        { recoverable: true, cause: fetchError },
   148→      );
   149→    }
   150→
   151→    const currentSettings = (currentEntity?.settings as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   152→    const currentToolSettings = (currentSettings[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   153→    const updatedToolSettings = deepMerge({}, currentToolSettings, patch);
   154→
   155→    throwIfAborted(signal);
   156→    const { error: rpcError } = await maybeAttachAbortSignal(
   157→      callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc(
   158→        tableName,
   159→        id,
   160→        toolId,
   161→        updatedToolSettings,
   162→      ),
   163→      signal,
   164→    );
   165→
   166→    if (rpcError) {
   167→      throw new ToolSettingsError(
   168→        'scope_fetch_failed',
   169→        `Failed to update ${scope} settings: ${rpcError.message}`,
   170→        { recoverable: true, cause: rpcError },
   171→      );
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    return updatedToolSettings;
   175→  } catch (error: unknown) {
   176→    if (isCancellationError(error)) {
   177→      throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   178→        recoverable: true,
   179→        cause: error,
   180→      });
   181→    }
   182→
   183→    throw error;
   184→  }
   185→}
   186→
   187→export function initializeToolSettingsWriteRuntime(): void {
   188→  initializeSettingsWriteQueue(rawUpdateToolSettings);
   189→}
   190→
   191→export function updateToolSettingsSupabase(
   192→  params: UpdateToolSettingsParams,
   193→  options: UpdateToolSettingsOptions = {},
   194→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
   195→  const { scope, id, toolId, patch } = params;
   196→  const signal = isAbortSignal(options.signal) ? options.signal : undefined;
   197→  const mode = isSettingsWriteMode(options.mode) ? options.mode : 'debounced';
   198→
   199→  return enqueueSettingsWrite({
   200→    scope,
   201→    entityId: id,
   202→    toolId,
   203→    patch: patch as Record<string, unknown>,
   204→    ...(signal ? { signal } : {}),
   205→  }, mode, rawUpdateToolSettings) as Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
   206→}
   207→

 src/tools/my-new-tool/

     5→export class CompletionError extends Error {

    14→    message: string;
    15→    context: string;

    18→    cause?: unknown;

    21→    this.name = 'CompletionError';
    22→    this.code = options.code;
    23→    this.recoverable = options.recoverable;
    24→    this.context = options.context;
    25→    this.metadata = options.metadata;
    26→    this.cause = options.cause;
    27→  }

    30→export function toCompletionError(
    31→  error: unknown,
    32→  options: {
    33→    code: string;
    34→    context: string;
    35→    recoverable: boolean;
    36→    message: string;

    45→      message: options.message,
    46→      metadata: {
    47→        ...(error.metadata ?? {}),
    48→        ...(options.metadata ?? {}),
    49→        wrapped_error_code: error.code,
    50→        wrapped_error_context: error.context,

1→import { toErrorMessage } from "../_shared/errorMessage.ts";
     2→import type { SupabaseClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2.49.4';
     3→import { edgeErrorResponse } from "../_shared/edgeRequest.ts";
     4→import type { CompletionLogger } from './types.ts';
     5→
     6→export interface TaskContext {
     7→  id: string;
     8→  task_type: string;
     9→  project_id: string;
    10→  params: Record<string, unknown>;
    11→  result_data: unknown;
    12→  tool_type: string;
    13→  category: string;
    14→  content_type: 'image' | 'video';
    15→  variant_type: string | null;
    16→}
    17→
    18→export type CompletionFollowUpStep =
    19→  | 'validation'
    20→  | 'orchestrator_completion'
    21→  | 'cost_calculation'
    22→  | 'follow_up_persistence';
    23→
    24→export interface CompletionFollowUpIssue {
    25→  step: CompletionFollowUpStep;
    26→  code: string;
    27→  message: string;
    28→}
    29→
    30→function defaultErrorCode(status: number): string {
    31→  if (status === 401) return 'authentication_failed';
    32→  if (status === 403) return 'forbidden';
    33→  if (status === 404) return 'not_found';
    34→  if (status === 405) return 'method_not_allowed';
    35→  if (status === 429) return 'rate_limited';
    36→  if (status === 503) return 'service_unavailable';
    37→  if (status >= 500) return 'internal_server_error';
    38→  return 'request_failed';
    39→}
    40→
    41→export function completeTaskErrorResponse(
    42→  message: string,
    43→  status: number,
    44→  errorCode = defaultErrorCode(status),
    45→  options?: { recoverable?: boolean },
    46→): Response {
    47→  return edgeErrorResponse(
    48→    {
    49→      errorCode,
    50→      message,
    51→      recoverable: options?.recoverable ?? (status >= 500 || status === 429),
    52→    },
    53→    status,
    54→  );
    55→}
    56→
    57→function asObjectRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
    58→  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
    59→    return {};
    60→  }
    61→  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
    62→}
    63→
    64→/**
    65→ * Best-effort mark a task as Failed so the UI can update immediately.
    66→ * Swallows errors — callers still return their own HTTP error response.
    67→ */
    68→export async function markTaskFailed(
    69→  supabase: SupabaseClient,
    70→  taskId: string,
    71→  errorMessage: string,
    72→): Promise<void> {
    73→  try {
    74→    await supabase.from("tasks").update({
    75→      status: "Failed",
    76→      error_message: errorMessage,
    77→      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    78→    }).eq("id", taskId).in("status", ["Queued", "In Progress"]);
    79→  } catch {
    80→    // Best-effort — don't mask the original error
    81→  }
    82→}
    83→
    84→export async function persistCompletionFollowUpIssues(
    85→  supabase: SupabaseClient,
    86→  taskId: string,
    87→  existingResultData: unknown,
    88→  issues: CompletionFollowUpIssue[],
    89→): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: unknown }> {
    90→  if (issues.length === 0) {
    91→    return { ok: true };
    92→  }
    93→
    94→  const baseResultData = asObjectRecord(existingResultData);
    95→  const completionFollowUp = {
    96→    status: 'degraded',
    97→    recorded_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    98→    issues,
    99→  };
   100→
   101→  const { error } = await supabase
   102→    .from('tasks')
   103→    .update({
   104→      result_data: {
   105→        ...baseResultData,
   106→        completion_follow_up: completionFollowUp,
   107→      },
   108→    })
   109→    .eq('id', taskId);
   110→
   111→  if (error) {
   112→    return { ok: false, error };
   113→  }
   114→
   115→  return { ok: true };
   116→}
   117→
   118→/**
   119→ * Fetch task context with all required fields for the completion flow.
   120→ * Uses a single query with FK join (tasks.task_type -> task_types.name).
   121→ * Returns null if task not found or on error.
   122→ */
   123→export async function fetchTaskContext(
   124→  supabase: SupabaseClient,
   125→  taskId: string,
   126→  logger?: CompletionLogger,
   127→): Promise<TaskContext | null> {
   128→  const { data: task, error } = await supabase
   129→    .from("tasks")
   130→    .select(`id, task_type, project_id, params, result_data, task_types!tasks_task_type_fkey(tool_type, category, content_type, variant_type)`)
   131→    .eq("id", taskId)
   132→    .single();
   133→
   134→  if (error || !task) {
   135→    logger?.error('Failed to fetch task context', {
   136→      task_id: taskId,
   137→      fetch_error: toErrorMessage(error),
   138→    });
   139→    return null;
   140→  }
   141→
   142→  const taskTypeInfo = task.task_types || {};
   143→
   144→  return {
   145→    id: task.id,
   146→    task_type: task.task_type,
   147→    project_id: task.project_id,
   148→    params: task.params || {},
   149→    result_data: task.result_data,
   150→    tool_type: taskTypeInfo.tool_type || 'unknown',
   151→    category: taskTypeInfo.category || 'unknown',
   152→    content_type: taskTypeInfo.content_type || 'image',
   153→    variant_type: taskTypeInfo.variant_type || null,
   154→  };
   155→}
   156→

1→// deno-lint-ignore-file
     2→import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.224.0/http/server.ts";
     3→import { buildOrchestratorRefOrFilter } from "../_shared/orchestratorReference.ts";
     4→import { withEdgeRequest } from "../_shared/edgeHandler.ts";
     5→import { authorizeTaskActor } from "../_shared/taskActorPolicy.ts";
     6→
     7→/**
     8→ * Edge function: get-orchestrator-children
     9→ *
    10→ * Fetches all child tasks for a given orchestrator task ID.
    11→ * Used by workers to get segment, stitch, and join tasks for an orchestrator.
    12→ *
    13→ * - Service-role key: can fetch any orchestrator's children
    14→ * - User token: can only fetch tasks from their own projects
    15→ *
    16→ * POST /functions/v1/get-orchestrator-children
    17→ * Headers: Authorization: Bearer <service-key or PAT>
    18→ * Body: { "orchestrator_task_id": "uuid" }
    19→ *
    20→ * Returns:
    21→ * - 200 OK with { tasks: [...] }
    22→ * - 400 Bad Request if orchestrator_task_id missing
    23→ * - 401 Unauthorized if no valid token
    24→ * - 403 Forbidden if user doesn't own the tasks' project
    25→ * - 500 Internal Server Error
    26→ */
    27→serve((req) => {
    28→  return withEdgeRequest(req, {
    29→    functionName: "get-orchestrator-children",
    30→    logPrefix: "[GET-ORCHESTRATOR-CHILDREN]",
    31→    parseBody: "strict",
    32→    errorResponseFormat: "text",
    33→    auth: {
    34→      required: true,
    35→    },
    36→  }, async ({ supabaseAdmin, logger, body: requestBody, auth }) => {
    37→  if (!auth) {
    38→    return new Response("Authentication failed", { status: 401 });
    39→  }
    40→
    41→  const orchestratorTaskId = typeof requestBody.orchestrator_task_id === "string"
    42→    ? requestBody.orchestrator_task_id
    43→    : null;
    44→  if (!orchestratorTaskId) {
    45→    logger.error("Missing orchestrator_task_id");
    46→    return new Response("orchestrator_task_id is required", { status: 400 });
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  // Set orchestrator task_id for logs
    50→  logger.setDefaultTaskId(orchestratorTaskId);
    51→
    52→  const actor = await authorizeTaskActor({
    53→    supabaseAdmin,
    54→    taskId: orchestratorTaskId,
    55→    auth,
    56→    logPrefix: "[GET-ORCHESTRATOR-CHILDREN]",
    57→  });
    58→  if (!actor.ok) {
    59→    logger.error("Task access denied", {
    60→      task_id: orchestratorTaskId,
    61→      error: actor.error,
    62→      status_code: actor.statusCode,
    63→    });
    64→    return new Response(actor.error, { status: actor.statusCode });
    65→  }
    66→  const isServiceRole = actor.value.isServiceRole;
    67→  let parentProjectId: string | null = null;
    68→
    69→  if (!isServiceRole) {
    70→    const { data: parentTask, error: parentTaskError } = await supabaseAdmin
    71→      .from("tasks")
    72→      .select("project_id")
    73→      .eq("id", orchestratorTaskId)
    74→      .single();
    75→
    76→    if (parentTaskError || !parentTask?.project_id) {
    77→      logger.error("Error resolving authorized parent task project", {
    78→        task_id: orchestratorTaskId,
    79→        error: parentTaskError?.message ?? "project_id missing",
    80→      });
    81→      return new Response("Error resolving parent task project", { status: 500 });
    82→    }
    83→
    84→    parentProjectId = parentTask.project_id;
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  // Query child tasks by orchestration contract + legacy orchestrator reference fields.
    88→  let childTasksQuery = supabaseAdmin
    89→    .from("tasks")
    90→    .select("id, task_type, status, params, output_location, project_id");
    91→
    92→  if (parentProjectId) {
    93→    childTasksQuery = childTasksQuery.eq("project_id", parentProjectId);
    94→  }
    95→
    96→  const { data: tasks, error: tasksError } = await childTasksQuery
    97→    .or([
    98→      buildOrchestratorRefOrFilter(orchestratorTaskId),
    99→      `params->>orchestrator_task_id.eq.${orchestratorTaskId}`,
   100→      `params->orchestrator_details->>orchestrator_task_id.eq.${orchestratorTaskId}`,
   101→    ].join(','))
   102→    .order("created_at", { ascending: true });
   103→
   104→  if (tasksError) {
   105→    logger.error("Error fetching child tasks", { error: tasksError.message });
   106→    return new Response(`Error fetching tasks: ${tasksError.message}`, { status: 500 });
   107→  }
   108→
   109→  // If no tasks found, return empty array
   110→  if (!tasks || tasks.length === 0) {
   111→    logger.info("No child tasks found", { orchestrator_task_id: orchestratorTaskId });
   112→    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ tasks: [] }), {
   113→      status: 200,
   114→      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
   115→    });
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  // Service role may access all tasks; user tokens are pre-authorized via taskActorPolicy above.
   119→  if (!isServiceRole) {
   120→    logger.debug("Caller access verified by taskActorPolicy", { task_id: orchestratorTaskId });
   121→  }
   122→
   123→  // Remove project_id from response (not needed by caller)
   124→  const tasksWithoutProjectId = tasks.map(({ project_id, ...rest }) => rest);
   125→
   126→  logger.info("Returning child tasks", { count: tasks.length });
   127→  return new Response(JSON.stringify({
   128→    tasks: tasksWithoutProjectId
   129→  }), {
   130→    status: 200,
   131→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
   132→  });
   133→});
   134→});
   135→
```

1→/**
     2→ * VideoEditPanel Component
     3→ *
     4→ * Unified video editing panel for both desktop and mobile layouts.
     5→ * Handles Trim, Replace Portion, and Regenerate sub-modes with variant display.
     6→ *
     7→ * Supports props-first pattern: optional state props (coreState, videoEditState,
     8→ * variantsState) override context values when provided. This allows the component
     9→ * to work both within VideoLightbox (using context) and standalone (using props).
    10→ */
    11→
    12→import React from 'react';
    13→import { Scissors, RefreshCw, RotateCcw, ArrowUp } from 'lucide-react';
    14→
    15→// Import video editing components
    16→import { TrimControlsPanel } from '@/shared/components/VideoTrimEditor/components/TrimControlsPanel';
    17→import type { TrimState } from '@/shared/types/videoTrim';
    18→import { VideoPortionEditor } from '@/shared/components/VideoPortionEditor';
    19→import { DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG } from '@/shared/lib/vaceDefaults';
    20→import type { UseVideoEditingReturn } from '../hooks/modes/types';
    21→import { EditPanelLayout } from './EditPanelLayout';
    22→import { ModeSelector } from './ModeSelector';
    23→import { SegmentRegenerateForm } from './SegmentRegenerateForm';
    24→import type { SegmentRegenerateFormProps } from './SegmentRegenerateForm';
    25→import { VideoEnhanceForm } from './VideoEnhanceForm';
    26→import type { VideoEnhanceSettings } from '../hooks/useVideoEnhance';
    27→import { useLightboxCoreSafe, useLightboxVariantsSafe } from '../contexts/LightboxStateContext';
    28→import { useVideoEditSafe } from '../contexts/VideoEditContext';
    29→
    30→interface VideoEditTrimControls {
    31→  // Trim mode controls (refs, handlers, save state)
    32→  trimState: TrimState;
    33→  onStartTrimChange: (seconds: number) => void;
    34→  onEndTrimChange: (seconds: number) => void;
    35→  onResetTrim: () => void;
    36→  trimmedDuration: number;
    37→  hasTrimChanges: boolean;
    38→  onSaveTrim: () => void;
    39→  isSavingTrim: boolean;
    40→  trimSaveProgress: number;
    41→  trimSaveError: string | null;
    42→  trimSaveSuccess: boolean;
    43→  videoUrl: string;
    44→  trimCurrentTime: number;
    45→  trimVideoRef: React.RefObject<HTMLVideoElement>;
    46→}
    47→
    48→interface VideoEditReplaceControls {
    49→  // Replace (portion) mode controls
    50→  videoEditing: UseVideoEditingReturn;
    51→  projectId: string | undefined;
    52→}
    53→
    54→interface VideoEditEnhanceControls {
    55→  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
    56→  onUpdateSetting: <K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
    57→    key: K,
    58→    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
    59→  ) => void;
    60→  onGenerate: () => void;
    61→  isGenerating?: boolean;
    62→  generateSuccess?: boolean;
    63→  canSubmit?: boolean;
    64→}
    65→
    66→interface VideoEditPanelProps {
    67→  /** Layout variant */
    68→  variant: 'desktop' | 'mobile';
    69→
    70→  /** Whether cloud mode is enabled (shows enhance mode) */
    71→  isCloudMode?: boolean;
    72→
    73→  trim: VideoEditTrimControls;
    74→  replace: VideoEditReplaceControls;
    75→
    76→  // Regenerate mode props - pass props instead of JSX for proper hook pattern
    77→  regenerateFormProps?: SegmentRegenerateFormProps | null;
    78→
    79→  // Enhance mode props (shown only when cloud mode is enabled)
    80→  enhance?: VideoEditEnhanceControls;
    81→
    82→  // Task ID for copy functionality
    83→  taskId?: string | null;
    84→}
    85→
    86→export const VideoEditPanel: React.FC<VideoEditPanelProps> = ({
    87→  variant,
    88→  isCloudMode,
    89→  trim,
    90→  replace,
    91→  // Regenerate props (specialized)
    92→  regenerateFormProps,
    93→  enhance,
    94→  // Task ID
    95→  taskId,
    96→}) => {
    97→  const contextCore = useLightboxCoreSafe();
    98→  const contextVideoEdit = useVideoEditSafe();
    99→  const contextVariants = useLightboxVariantsSafe();
   100→
   101→  const { onClose } = contextCore;
   102→
   103→  const {
   104→    videoEditSubMode,
   105→    handleEnterVideoTrimMode: onEnterTrimMode,
   106→    handleEnterVideoReplaceMode: onEnterReplaceMode,
   107→    handleEnterVideoRegenerateMode: onEnterRegenerateMode,
   108→    handleEnterVideoEnhanceMode: onEnterEnhanceMode,
   109→    handleExitVideoEditMode: onExitVideoEditMode,
   110→  } = contextVideoEdit;
   111→
   112→  const {
   113→    variants,
   114→    activeVariant,
   115→    handleVariantSelect: onVariantSelect,
   116→    handleMakePrimary: onMakePrimary,
   117→    isLoadingVariants,
   118→    onLoadVariantSettings,
   119→    handleDeleteVariant: onDeleteVariant,
   120→  } = contextVariants;
   121→
   122→  const activeVariantId = activeVariant?.id || null;
   123→  // Mode selector items for video editing
   124→  const modeSelectorItems = [
   125→    {
   126→      id: 'trim',
   127→      label: 'Trim',
   128→      icon: <Scissors />,
   129→      onClick: onEnterTrimMode,
   130→    },
   131→    {
   132→      id: 'replace',
   133→      label: 'Replace',
   134→      icon: <RefreshCw />,
   135→      onClick: onEnterReplaceMode,
   136→    },
   137→    ...(regenerateFormProps ? [{
   138→      id: 'regenerate',
   139→      label: 'Regenerate',
   140→      icon: <RotateCcw />,
   141→      onClick: onEnterRegenerateMode,
   142→    }] : []),
   143→    // Enhance mode - only shown when cloud mode is enabled
   144→    ...(isCloudMode ? [{
   145→      id: 'enhance',
   146→      label: 'Enhance',
   147→      icon: <ArrowUp />,
   148→      onClick: onEnterEnhanceMode,
   149→    }] : []),
   150→  ];
   151→
   152→  const modeSelector = (
   153→    <ModeSelector
   154→      items={modeSelectorItems}
   155→      activeId={videoEditSubMode ?? 'trim'}
   156→    />
   157→  );
   158→
   159→  return (
   160→    <EditPanelLayout
   161→      variant={variant}
   162→      onClose={onClose}
   163→      onExitEditMode={onExitVideoEditMode}
   164→      modeSelector={modeSelector}
   165→      taskId={taskId}
   166→      variants={variants}
   167→      activeVariantId={activeVariantId}
   168→      onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}
   169→      onMakePrimary={onMakePrimary}
   170→      isLoadingVariants={isLoadingVariants}
   171→      onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   172→      onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant}
   173→    >
   174→      {/* Sub-mode content */}
   175→      {videoEditSubMode === 'trim' && (
   176→        <TrimControlsPanel
   177→          trimState={trim.trimState}
   178→          onStartTrimChange={trim.onStartTrimChange}
   179→          onEndTrimChange={trim.onEndTrimChange}
   180→          onResetTrim={trim.onResetTrim}
   181→          trimmedDuration={trim.trimmedDuration}
   182→          hasTrimChanges={trim.hasTrimChanges}
   183→          onSave={trim.onSaveTrim}
   184→          isSaving={trim.isSavingTrim}
   185→          saveProgress={trim.trimSaveProgress}
   186→          saveError={trim.trimSaveError}
   187→          saveSuccess={trim.trimSaveSuccess}
   188→          onClose={onClose}
   189→          variant={variant}
   190→          videoUrl={trim.videoUrl}
   191→          currentTime={trim.trimCurrentTime}
   192→          videoRef={trim.trimVideoRef}
   193→          hideHeader
   194→        />
   195→      )}
   196→      {videoEditSubMode === 'replace' && (
   197→        <VideoPortionEditor
   198→          settings={{
   199→            gapFrames: replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.gapFrameCount,
   200→            setGapFrames: (val) => replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateField('gapFrameCount', val),
   201→            contextFrames: replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.contextFrameCount,
   202→            setContextFrames: (val) => {
   203→              const maxGap = Math.max(1, 81 - (val * 2));
   204→              const gapFrames = replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.gapFrameCount;
   205→              const newGapFrames = gapFrames > maxGap ? maxGap : gapFrames;
   206→              replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateFields({
   207→                contextFrameCount: val,
   208→                gapFrameCount: newGapFrames
   209→              });
   210→            },
   211→            maxContextFrames: replace.videoEditing.maxContextFrames,
   212→            negativePrompt: replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.negativePrompt || '',
   213→            setNegativePrompt: (val) => replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateField('negativePrompt', val),
   214→            enhancePrompt: replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.enhancePrompt,
   215→            setEnhancePrompt: (val) => replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateField('enhancePrompt', val),
   216→          }}
   217→          selections={{
   218→            selections: replace.videoEditing.selections,
   219→            onUpdateSelectionSettings: replace.videoEditing.handleUpdateSelectionSettings,
   220→            onAddSelection: replace.videoEditing.handleAddSelection,
   221→            onRemoveSelection: replace.videoEditing.handleRemoveSelection,
   222→            videoUrl: trim.videoUrl,
   223→            fps: 16,
   224→          }}
   225→          lora={{
   226→            availableLoras: replace.videoEditing.availableLoras,
   227→            projectId: replace.projectId ?? null,
   228→            loraManager: replace.videoEditing.loraManager,
   229→          }}
   230→          motion={{
   231→            motionMode: (replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.motionMode || 'basic') as 'basic' | 'advanced',
   232→            onMotionModeChange: (mode) => replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateField('motionMode', mode),
   233→            phaseConfig: replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.phaseConfig ?? DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG,
   234→            onPhaseConfigChange: (config) => replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateField('phaseConfig', config),
   235→            randomSeed: replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.randomSeed ?? true,
   236→            onRandomSeedChange: (val) => replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateField('randomSeed', val),
   237→            selectedPhasePresetId: replace.videoEditing.editSettings.settings.selectedPhasePresetId ?? null,
   238→            onPhasePresetSelect: (presetId, config) => {
   239→              replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateFields({
   240→                selectedPhasePresetId: presetId,
   241→                phaseConfig: config,
   242→              });
   243→            },
   244→            onPhasePresetRemove: () => {
   245→              replace.videoEditing.editSettings.updateField('selectedPhasePresetId', null);
   246→            },
   247→          }}
   248→          actions={{
   249→            onGenerate: replace.videoEditing.handleGenerate,
   250→            isGenerating: replace.videoEditing.isGenerating,
   251→            generateSuccess: replace.videoEditing.generateSuccess,
   252→            isGenerateDisabled: !replace.videoEditing.isValid,
   253→            validationErrors: replace.videoEditing.validationErrors,
   254→          }}
   255→          stateOverrides={{ hideHeader: true }}
   256→        />
   257→      )}
   258→      {videoEditSubMode === 'regenerate' && regenerateFormProps && (
   259→        <SegmentRegenerateForm {...regenerateFormProps} />
   260→      )}
   261→      {videoEditSubMode === 'enhance' && enhance && (
   262→        <VideoEnhanceForm
   263→          settings={enhance.settings}
   264→          onUpdateSetting={enhance.onUpdateSetting}
   265→          onGenerate={enhance.onGenerate}
   266→          isGenerating={enhance.isGenerating ?? false}
   267→          generateSuccess={enhance.generateSuccess ?? false}
   268→          canSubmit={enhance.canSubmit ?? false}
   269→          variant={variant}
   270→          videoUrl={trim.videoUrl}
   271→        />
   272→      )}
   273→    </EditPanelLayout>
   274→  );
   275→};
   276→

   149→ * @param useVaceModel - Whether to use VACE model (determines base config)
   150→ * @param _amountOfMotion - 0-1 motion value (NOT 0-100)
   151→ * @param userLoras - User-selected LoRAs to add to each phase
   152→ * @returns PhaseConfig with base LoRAs + motion LoRA + user LoRAs
   153→ */
   154→export function buildBasicModePhaseConfig(
   155→  useVaceModel: boolean,
   156→  _amountOfMotion: number,
   157→  userLoras: UserLoraInput[]
   158→): PhaseConfig {
   159→  const baseConfig = useVaceModel ? DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG : DEFAULT_PHASE_CONFIG;
   160→  const totalPhases = baseConfig.phases.length;
   161→
   162→  return {
   163→    ...baseConfig,
   164→    steps_per_phase: [...baseConfig.steps_per_phase],
   165→    phases: baseConfig.phases.map((phase, phaseIndex) => {
   166→      const isLastPhase = phaseIndex === totalPhases - 1;
   167→      const additionalLoras: PhaseLoraConfig[] = [];
   168→
   169→      // Add user-selected LoRAs with multi-stage support
   170→      userLoras.forEach(lora => {
   171→        if (lora.isMultiStage) {
   172→          // Multi-stage LoRA: route to correct phase based on noise level
   173→          if (isLastPhase) {
   174→            // Final phase: use low_noise LoRA if available
   175→            if (lora.lowNoisePath) {
   176→              additionalLoras.push({
   177→                url: lora.lowNoisePath,
   178→                multiplier: lora.strength.toFixed(2)
   179→              });
   180→            }
   181→          } else {
   182→            // Early phases: use high_noise LoRA (main path)
   183→            if (lora.path) {
   184→              additionalLoras.push({
   185→                url: lora.path,
   186→                multiplier: lora.strength.toFixed(2)
   187→              });
   188→            }
   189→          }
   190→        } else {
   191→          // Single-stage LoRA: apply to all phases
   192→          if (lora.path) {
   193→            additionalLoras.push({
   194→              url: lora.path,
   195→              multiplier: lora.strength.toFixed(2)
   196→            });
   197→          }
   198→        }
   199→      });
   200→
   201→      return {
   202→        ...phase,
   203→        loras: [
   204→          ...phase.loras.map(l => ({ ...l })), // Deep clone base LoRAs
   205→          ...additionalLoras
   206→        ]
   207→      };
   208→    })
   209→  };
   210→}
   211→
```

    48→export const InfoPanel: React.FC<InfoPanelProps> = ({

   153→          />
   154→        </div>

   158→
   159→  // Render task details wrapper
   160→  const renderTaskDetails = () => (
   161→    <TaskDetailsPanelWrapper
   162→      taskDetailsData={taskDetailsData}
   163→      replaceImages={replaceImages}
   164→      onReplaceImagesChange={onReplaceImagesChange}
   165→      onClose={onClose}
   166→    />
   167→  );
   168→
   169→  // Render variants section - matches EditPanelLayout styling
   170→  const renderVariants = () => {
   171→    if (!hasVariants) return null;
   172→
   173→    // Match EditPanelLayout: border-t, consistent padding
   174→    const variantPadding = isMobile ? 'pt-2 mt-2 px-3 pb-2' : 'pt-4 mt-4 p-6';
   175→
   176→    return (
   177→      <div
   178→        ref={variantsSectionRef}
   179→        className={cn("border-t border-border", variantPadding)}
   180→      >
   181→        <VariantSelector
   182→          variants={variants}
   183→          activeVariantId={activeVariant?.id || null}
   184→          onVariantSelect={onVariantSelect}

   188→          isPromoting={isPromoting}
   189→          onDeleteVariant={onDeleteVariant}
   190→          onLoadVariantSettings={onLoadVariantSettings}
   191→          onLoadVariantImages={onLoadVariantImages}
   192→          currentSegmentImages={currentSegmentImages}
   193→          readOnly={readOnly}
   194→        />
   195→      </div>
   196→    );
   197→  };
   198→
   199→  // Both desktop and mobile: variants inside scroll area (matches EditPanelLayout)
   200→  return (

   202→      {renderHeader()}
   203→
   204→      {/* Scrollable content area - contains both task details and variants */}

   206→        {renderTaskDetails()}
   207→        {renderVariants()}
   208→      </div>
   209→    </div>
   210→  );
   211→};

1→/**
     2→ * Handles task generation for inpainting and annotated edits.
     3→ * Exports mask from Konva, uploads to storage, and creates tasks.
     4→ */
     5→
     6→import { useCallback } from 'react';
     7→import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
     8→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     9→import type { StrokeOverlayHandle } from '../../components/StrokeOverlay';
    10→import type { BrushStroke, EditAdvancedSettings, QwenEditModel } from './types';
    11→import { getGenerationId } from '@/shared/lib/media/mediaTypeHelpers';
    12→import { useTaskPlaceholder } from '@/shared/hooks/tasks/useTaskPlaceholder';
    13→import { createInpaintingTaskWorkflow } from './createInpaintingTaskWorkflow';
    14→
    15→/**
    16→ * Task type configuration - captures the differences between inpaint and annotate modes
    17→ */
    18→type TaskType = 'inpaint' | 'annotate';
    19→
    20→interface TaskTypeConfig {
    21→  emptyStrokesError: string;
    22→  overlayNotReadyError: string;
    23→  taskCreationError: string;
    24→}
    25→
    26→const TASK_CONFIGS: Record<TaskType, TaskTypeConfig> = {
    27→  inpaint: {
    28→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please paint on the image first',
    29→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Paint overlay not ready',
    30→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create inpaint task',
    31→  },
    32→  annotate: {
    33→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please draw an annotation rectangle',
    34→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Annotation overlay not ready',
    35→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create annotated edit task',
    36→  },
    37→};
    38→
    39→interface UseTaskGenerationProps {
    40→  media: GenerationRow;
    41→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    42→  shotId?: string;
    43→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    44→  isVideo: boolean;
    45→  loras?: Array<{ url: string; strength: number }>;
    46→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    47→  activeVariantLocation?: string | null;
    48→  createAsGeneration?: boolean;
    49→  advancedSettings?: EditAdvancedSettings;
    50→  qwenEditModel?: QwenEditModel;
    51→  // State
    52→  inpaintStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    53→  annotationStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    54→  inpaintPrompt: string;
    55→  inpaintNumGenerations: number;
    56→  // Refs
    57→  strokeOverlayRef: React.RefObject<StrokeOverlayHandle>;
    58→  // Callbacks
    59→  handleExitInpaintMode: () => void;
    60→  setIsGeneratingInpaint: (isGenerating: boolean) => void;
    61→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess: (success: boolean) => void;
    62→}
    63→
    64→export function useTaskGeneration({
    65→  media,
    66→  selectedProjectId,
    67→  shotId,
    68→  toolTypeOverride,
    69→  isVideo,
    70→  loras,
    71→  activeVariantId,
    72→  activeVariantLocation,
    73→  createAsGeneration,
    74→  advancedSettings,
    75→  qwenEditModel,
    76→  inpaintStrokes,
    77→  annotationStrokes,
    78→  inpaintPrompt,
    79→  inpaintNumGenerations,
    80→  strokeOverlayRef,
    81→  handleExitInpaintMode,
    82→  setIsGeneratingInpaint,
    83→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess,
    84→}: UseTaskGenerationProps) {
    85→  // Get actual generation ID (may differ from media.id for shot_generations)
    86→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
    87→  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
    88→
    89→  /**
    90→   * Unified task generation function
    91→   * Handles both inpaint and annotate modes with mode-specific configuration
    92→   */
    93→  const handleGenerateTask = useCallback(async (taskType: TaskType) => {
    94→    const config = TASK_CONFIGS[taskType];
    95→    const strokes = taskType === 'inpaint' ? inpaintStrokes : annotationStrokes;
    96→
    97→    // Validation
    98→    if (!selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
    99→      toast.error(`Cannot generate ${taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'inpaint' : 'annotated edit'}`);
   100→      return;
   101→    }
   102→
   103→    if (strokes.length === 0) {
   104→      toast.error(config.emptyStrokesError);
   105→      return;
   106→    }
   107→
   108→    if (!inpaintPrompt.trim()) {
   109→      toast.error('Please enter a prompt');
   110→      return;
   111→    }
   112→
   113→    if (!strokeOverlayRef.current) {
   114→      toast.error(config.overlayNotReadyError);
   115→      return;
   116→    }
   117→
   118→    const incomingTaskType = taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'image_inpaint' : 'annotated_image_edit';
   119→
   120→    setIsGeneratingInpaint(true);
   121→    try {
   122→      await run({
   123→        taskType: incomingTaskType,
   124→        label: inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'Editing...',
   125→        context: 'useTaskGeneration',
   126→        toastTitle: config.taskCreationError,
   127→        create: async () => {
   128→          if (!actualGenerationId) {
   129→            throw new Error('Missing generation id');
   130→          }
   131→
   132→          return createInpaintingTaskWorkflow({
   133→            taskType,
   134→            media,
   135→            selectedProjectId,
   136→            shotId,
   137→            toolTypeOverride,
   138→            loras,
   139→            activeVariantId,
   140→            activeVariantLocation,
   141→            createAsGeneration,
   142→            advancedSettings,
   143→            qwenEditModel,
   144→            inpaintPrompt,
   145→            inpaintNumGenerations,
   146→            actualGenerationId,
   147→            strokeOverlay: strokeOverlayRef.current!,
   148→          });
   149→        },
   150→        onSuccess: () => {
   151→          // Show success state
   152→          setInpaintGenerateSuccess(true);
   153→
   154→          // Wait 1 second to show success, then exit
   155→          setTimeout(() => {
   156→            setInpaintGenerateSuccess(false);
   157→            handleExitInpaintMode();
   158→          }, 1000);
   159→        },
   160→      });
   161→    } finally {
   162→      setIsGeneratingInpaint(false);
   163→    }
   164→  }, [
   165→    selectedProjectId, isVideo, inpaintStrokes, annotationStrokes, inpaintPrompt, inpaintNumGenerations,
   166→    media, handleExitInpaintMode, shotId, toolTypeOverride, loras,
   167→    activeVariantLocation, activeVariantId, createAsGeneration, advancedSettings,
   168→    qwenEditModel, strokeOverlayRef, actualGenerationId,
   169→    setIsGeneratingInpaint, setInpaintGenerateSuccess, run
   170→  ]);
   171→
   172→  // Stable callbacks that preserve the original API
   173→  const handleGenerateInpaint = useCallback(() => {
   174→    return handleGenerateTask('inpaint');
   175→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   176→
   177→  const handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit = useCallback(() => {
   178→    return handleGenerateTask('annotate');
   179→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   180→
   181→  return {
   182→    handleGenerateInpaint,
   183→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,
   184→  };
   185→}
   186→
```

1→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     2→import type { Database } from '@/integrations/supabase/databasePublicTypes';
     3→import { coerceGenerationRowDto, mapGenerationRowDtoToRow } from '@/domains/generation/mappers/generationRowMapper';
     4→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     5→import {
     6→  createRepositoryQueryError,
     7→  createInvalidRowShapeError,
     8→  isRepositoryNoRowsError,
     9→} from './repositoryErrors';
    10→
    11→type GenerationRecord = Database['public']['Tables']['generations']['Row'] & Record<string, unknown>;
    12→
    13→export async function fetchGenerationById(generationId: string): Promise<GenerationRow | null> {
    14→  const { data, error } = await supabase().from('generations')
    15→    .select('*')
    16→    .eq('id', generationId)
    17→    .maybeSingle();
    18→
    19→  if (error) {
    20→    if (isRepositoryNoRowsError(error)) {
    21→      return null;
    22→    }
    23→    throw createRepositoryQueryError('generation', error, { generationId });
    24→  }
    25→
    26→  if (!data) {
    27→    return null;
    28→  }
    29→
    30→  const row = coerceGenerationRowDto(data);
    31→  if (!row) {
    32→    throw createInvalidRowShapeError('generation', { generationId });
    33→  }
    34→
    35→  return mapGenerationRowDtoToRow(row);
    36→}
    37→
    38→export async function fetchGenerationRecordById(generationId: string): Promise<GenerationRecord | null> {
    39→  const { data, error } = await supabase().from('generations')
    40→    .select('*')
    41→    .eq('id', generationId)
    42→    .maybeSingle();
    43→
    44→  if (error) {
    45→    if (isRepositoryNoRowsError(error)) {
    46→      return null;
    47→    }
    48→    throw createRepositoryQueryError('generation record', error, { generationId });
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  if (!data) {
    52→    return null;
    53→  }
    54→
    55→  return data as GenerationRecord;
    56→}
    57→

   430→+
   431→+function useShotEditorBootstrap({
   432→+  selectedShotId,
   433→+  projectId,
   434→+  optimisticShotData,
   435→+}: Pick<ShotEditorProps, 'selectedShotId' | 'projectId' | 'optimisticShotData'>): ShotEditorBootstrapResult {
   436→+  const promptSettings = usePromptSettings();

   438→+  const frameSettings = useFrameSettings();
   439→+  const phaseConfigSettings = usePhaseConfigSettings();
   440→+  const generationModeSettings = useGenerationModeSettings();
   441→+  const steerableMotionSettings = useSteerableMotionSettings();
   442→+  const loraSettings = useLoraSettings();
   443→+  const { isLoading: settingsLoadingFromContext } = useVideoTravelSettings();
   444→+
   445→+  const shotSetup = useShotEditorSetup({

   447→+    projectId,
   448→+    optimisticShotData: optimisticShotData as Shot | undefined,
   449→+    batchVideoFrames: frameSettings.batchVideoFrames,
   450→+  });
   451→+
   452→+  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   453→+  const { setCurrentShotId } = useCurrentShot();
   454→+  const { navigateToShot } = useShotNavigation();
   455→+  const { createShot } = useShotCreation();
   456→+  const addImageToShotMutation = useAddImageToShot();
   457→+  const removeImageFromShotMutation = useRemoveImageFromShot();
   458→+  const updateShotImageOrderMutation = useUpdateShotImageOrder();
   459→+  const { mutateAsync: addToShotMutation, mutateAsyncWithoutPosition: addToShotWithoutPositionMutation } =
   460→+    addImageToShotMutation;
   461→+
   462→+  const createShotRef = useRef(createShot);
   463→+  createShotRef.current = createShot;
   464→+  const addToShotMutationRef = useRef(addToShotMutation);
   465→+  addToShotMutationRef.current = addToShotMutation;
   466→+  const addToShotWithoutPositionMutationRef = useRef(addToShotWithoutPositionMutation);
   467→+  addToShotWithoutPositionMutationRef.current = addToShotWithoutPositionMutation;
   468→+
   469→+  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
   470→+  const { isPhone, aspectAdjustedColumns } = useAspectAdjustedColumns(shotSetup.effectiveAspectRatio);
   471→+  const { setIsGenerationsPaneLocked } = usePanes();
   472→+  const lastVideoGeneration = useLastVideoGeneration(selectedShotId);
   473→+
   474→+  return {
   475→+    promptSettings,
   476→+    motionSettings,
   477→+    frameSettings,
   478→+    phaseConfigSettings,
   479→+    generationModeSettings,
   480→+    steerableMotionSettings,

   489→+    timelineImages: shotSetup.timelineImages,
   490→+    unpositionedImages: shotSetup.unpositionedImages,
   491→+    videoOutputs: shotSetup.videoOutputs,
   492→+    contextImages: shotSetup.contextImages,

  1120→+function normalizeShotLoras(value: unknown): ShotLora[] {
  1121→+  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {

  1134→+      return [];

  1177→+function normalizeStructureVideo(value: unknown): VideoTravelSettings['structureVideo'] {
  1178→+  const record = asRecord(value);

  1186→+        motionStrength: asFiniteNumber(record.motionStrength) ?? DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.motionStrength,

  1191→+    }

  1198→+    defaultStructureType: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.structureType,
  1199→+    defaultUni3cEndPercent: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.uni3cEndPercent,

  1201→+  if (!migrated) {

  1203→+  }

  1209→+    motionStrength: migrated.motion_strength ?? DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.motionStrength,
  1210→+    ...(migrated.structure_type ? { structureType: migrated.structure_type } : {}),
  1211→+  };

  1214→+export function normalizeVideoTravelSettings(value: unknown): VideoTravelSettings {
  1215→+  const defaults = cloneVideoTravelDefaults();
  1216→+  const record = asRecord(value);

  1220→+
  1221→+  return {
  1222→+    ...defaults,
  1223→+    prompt: asString(record.prompt) ?? defaults.prompt,

  1225→+    batchVideoFrames: asFiniteNumber(record.batchVideoFrames) ?? defaults.batchVideoFrames,
  1226→+    batchVideoSteps: asFiniteNumber(record.batchVideoSteps) ?? defaults.batchVideoSteps,
  1227→+    dimensionSource: asEnum(record.dimensionSource, ['project', 'firstImage', 'custom']) ?? defaults.dimensionSource,

  1229→+    customHeight: asFiniteNumber(record.customHeight) ?? defaults.customHeight,

  1232→+      ...(asRecord(record.steerableMotionSettings) ?? {}),

  1238→+    amountOfMotion: asFiniteNumber(record.amountOfMotion) ?? defaults.amountOfMotion,

  1240→+    advancedMode: asBoolean(record.advancedMode) ?? defaults.advancedMode,
  1241→+    phaseConfig: (asRecord(record.phaseConfig) as PhaseConfig | null) ?? defaults.phaseConfig,

  1253→+  };

   105→export function collectTravelStructureLegacyUsage(
   106→  value: unknown,
   107→): TravelStructureLegacyUsage {
   108→  const record = value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
   109→    ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
   110→    : {};
   111→
   112→  const topLevelFields = LEGACY_TRAVEL_TOP_LEVEL_FIELDS.filter(
   113→    (field) => record[field] !== undefined,
   114→  );
   115→
   116→  const structureVideos = Array.isArray(record.structure_videos)
   117→    ? record.structure_videos
   118→    : [];
   119→  const structureVideoFields = LEGACY_TRAVEL_STRUCTURE_VIDEO_FIELDS.filter((field) =>
   120→    structureVideos.some((entry) => hasLegacyField(entry, field)),
   121→  );
   122→
   123→  return { topLevelFields, structureVideoFields };
   124→}
   125→
   126→export function enforceTravelStructureLegacyPolicy(
   127→  usage: TravelStructureLegacyUsage,
   128→  options?: {
   129→    context?: string;
   130→    enforcement?: 'warn' | 'throw' | 'auto';
   131→  },
   132→): boolean {
   133→  const usedFields = [...usage.topLevelFields, ...usage.structureVideoFields];
   134→  if (usedFields.length === 0) {

   136→  }
   137→

   141→    policy: legacyStructurePolicy,
   142→    remediation: `Migrate to structure_guidance + canonical structure_videos. Legacy fields seen: ${details}.` +

   144→    enforcement: options?.enforcement ?? 'auto',
   145→  });
