Here is my detailed assessment of all uncommitted changes in the `supabase/` directory.

---

## File 1: `supabase/functions/_shared/rateLimit.ts`

**Change**: JSDoc comment fix — `@param serviceUnavailableResponse` changed to `@param input.responses.serviceUnavailable`.

**Assessment**: Pure documentation fix, no logic change. Correct. No issues.

---

## File 2: `supabase/functions/complete_task/completionHelpers.ts`

**Change**: Added an optional `options?: { recoverable?: boolean }` parameter to `completeTaskErrorResponse`, allowing callers to override the default `recoverable` heuristic (`status >= 500 || status === 429`).

**Assessment**:
- **Correctness**: Good. The `options?.recoverable ?? (default)` pattern preserves backward compatibility — existing callers without the argument get the same behavior.
- **Edge cases**: If `options` is passed but `recoverable` is omitted (e.g., `{ }` with no `recoverable`), it falls through to the default, which is correct.
- **No issues found.**

---

## File 3: `supabase/functions/complete_task/handler.ts`

**Change**: The `catch (genErr)` block now distinguishes `CompletionError` from generic errors. For `CompletionError`, it:
1. Logs structured fields (`error_code`, `recoverable`, `error_context`, `error_metadata`)
2. Includes the error code in the `markTaskFailed` message
3. Passes the structured error's `message`, `code`, and `recoverable` to `completeTaskErrorResponse` instead of the generic "Internal server error"

**Assessment**:
- **Correctness**: Sound. The `instanceof` check is appropriate since `CompletionError` is a class extending `Error`. The fallback for non-`CompletionError` exceptions preserves the existing behavior.
- **Potential concern — information leakage**: When a `CompletionError` is caught, its `message` is returned directly to the client (`normalizedError?.message ?? "Internal server error"`). This is intentional (the `CompletionError` class is designed to carry user-facing messages), but worth noting: any code throwing `CompletionError` must ensure the `message` is safe to expose. Since this is a structured error class under the project's control, this is fine as long as it's a known convention.
- **The `error` field still logs the raw `toErrorMessage(genErr)` string**, even for `CompletionError`. This means the structured log entry includes both `error` (stringified) and `error_code`/`error_context` (structured). Slight redundancy but not harmful — helps with plain-text log searching.
- **No issues found.**

---

## File 4: `supabase/functions/complete_task/handler.test.ts`

**Change**: Two new test cases added:
1. `CompletionError` is correctly forwarded with structured details (code, recoverable, context, metadata)
2. Plain `Error` still produces the generic "Internal server error" response

**Assessment**:
- **Coverage**: Good. Both the happy path (structured error) and fallback (plain error) are tested. The assertions check the response body, logger calls, `markTaskFailed` arguments, and `completeTaskErrorResponse` arguments.
- **Minor observation**: The mock for `completeTaskErrorResponse` was updated to pass through `errorCode` and `options` into the response JSON, which makes the assertions work. This is correct for test purposes.
- **One subtle thing**: In the "wraps plain generation failures" test, the assertion checks `recoverable: undefined` in the response JSON. This is because `completeTaskErrorResponse` is called without the `options` parameter for plain errors, and the mock serializes `undefined`. In the real `completeTaskErrorResponse`, the default logic would apply (`status >= 500 → true`). The test is testing the mock interaction rather than the real behavior, which is appropriate for a unit test of `handler.ts`.
- **No issues found.**

---

## File 5: `supabase/functions/get-orchestrator-children/index.ts`

**Change**: For non-service-role callers, the function now:
1. Fetches the parent task's `project_id`
2. Adds `.eq("project_id", parentProjectId)` to the child tasks query

This scopes child task results to only those sharing the same project as the orchestrator task.

**Assessment**:
- **Correctness**: The logic is correct. `authorizeTaskActor` already verified the user can access the orchestrator task, so fetching the parent task's `project_id` and filtering children by it is a sound authorization pattern.
- **Duplicate DB call**: `authorizeTaskActor` likely already fetches the orchestrator task from the DB to verify access. The new code makes a second query for the same task to get `project_id`. This is a minor inefficiency. If `authorizeTaskActor` returned the task data (or at least `project_id`), this extra query could be avoided. Not a bug, but worth noting for a future cleanup.
- **Security improvement**: Previously, a user-token caller could see child tasks from any project as long as they could access the orchestrator task. Now the child tasks are scoped to the same project. This is a meaningful authorization tightening.
- **Edge case — `project_id` is null on the parent task**: The code handles this with `!parentTask?.project_id` check and returns a 500. This is correct since tasks should always have a `project_id`.
- **The `logger.debug` call on line 120** is why the test file needed the `loggerDebugMock` addition — consistent.
- **No issues found.**

---

## File 6: `supabase/functions/get-orchestrator-children/index.test.ts`

**Change**: Added `loggerDebugMock` to the mock logger so the new `logger.debug(...)` call in the implementation doesn't throw.

**Assessment**: Mechanical mock addition. Correct. No issues.

---

## Summary

All changes are correct and well-structured. No bugs, security issues, or missed edge cases found. Key observations:

1. **`complete_task` error handling**: Clean enhancement. `CompletionError` provides structured error propagation without breaking existing behavior for plain errors. Test coverage is good.

2. **`get-orchestrator-children` authorization**: Meaningful security improvement scoping child tasks by `project_id` for user-token callers. One minor inefficiency: the parent task is queried twice (once in `authorizeTaskActor`, once for `project_id`). This could be consolidated in a future pass but is not a problem now.

3. **`rateLimit.ts`**: Trivial JSDoc fix. Correct.

4. **Test coverage**: Both changed edge functions have corresponding test updates. The `CompletionError` path has both positive and negative test cases.

No items flagged as requiring changes before merge.

> AGENT

No changes to that file -- so it's an existing file that was NOT updated when the API signature changed. This is a **regression bug**: `useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts` line 205 passes `'immediate'` as the second positional argument, but the new signature expects `UpdateToolSettingsOptions` (an object). The string `'immediate'` will be assigned to `options`, and `isSettingsWriteMode(options.mode)` will be `false`, so the mode will silently fall back to `'debounced'` instead of `'immediate'`. This is a correctness bug -- flushes on unmount/navigation will be debounced instead of immediate.

The interface `ImageLightboxControlsPanelProps` still declares four props that are no longer destructured in the component:
- `shotId` (line 15)
- `onOpenExternalGeneration` (line 16)
- `shotWorkflow` (line 17)
- `sharedModel` (line 20)

These are removed from the destructuring (correct) but left in the interface. This means **TypeScript won't error** — the props are optional or still typed — but the caller at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/domains/media-lightbox/ImageLightbox.tsx` (lines 276-281) is still passing them:
```tsx
shotId={props.shotWorkflow?.shotId}
onOpenExternalGeneration={props.onOpenExternalGeneration}
```

These passed values are silently ignored. The interface props and the caller's pass-throughs should both be cleaned up.

**Issue B: Stale imports (lines 4-5, 10)**
`LightboxShotWorkflowProps` (line 5) and `ImageLightboxSharedModel` (line 10) are only used in the interface for the dead props. If the dead props are removed, these imports become dead too.

## Assessment of Uncommitted Changes

### 1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/MediaGallery.tsx`

**Change**: Replaces a 2-line deprecated shim (`export { MediaGallery } from '@/features/gallery/...'`) with the full ~487-line orchestrator component, moving canonical ownership back to `src/shared/`.

**Quality assessment**: Well-structured. The component is large but appropriately orchestrated -- it composes many hooks (state, filters, pagination, actions, lightbox session, view interactions) and renders sub-components (Header, ShotNotifier, Grid, Lightbox, MobileBottomBar). The `useAspectRatioLayout` helper is correctly extracted as a local function at the top of the file.

**Potential concerns**:
- Two destructured props are unused: `_currentToolTypeName` and `_lastShotNameForTooltip` (lines ~95, ~101). These are prefixed with `_` which is the standard convention for intentionally unused destructured props, so this is fine.
- The `withPaginationReset` callback (line ~147) captures `paginationHook` in its dependency array. Since `paginationHook` is a hook return object that changes every render, this callback will re-create every render. However, it's only used in event handlers (not in dependency arrays of other effects), so this is acceptable -- it's not causing cascading re-renders.
- No dead code, no missing imports. All sub-component and hook imports resolve to files within the same `MediaGallery/` directory tree.

**Verdict**: Correct. No regressions.

### 2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/hooks/useMediaGalleryLightboxControllers.ts`

**Change**: Replaces direct `getSupabaseClient().from("generations").select("*").eq(...).single()` call with `fetchGenerationRecordById(generationId)` from the repository layer.

**Quality assessment**: Clean facade migration. The repository function (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/integrations/supabase/repositories/generationRepository.ts`) uses `.maybeSingle()` (returns null instead of error for no rows) and throws on actual errors. The calling code correctly:
- Checks `if (!row)` for the not-found case
- Relies on the outer `try/catch` for error cases (the repository throws)
- Removes the intermediate `const row = data as Record<string, unknown>` cast since the repository return type already includes `Record<string, unknown>`

**Verdict**: Correct. Cleaner than before.

This is a behavior change worth noting. Previously, if `sourceSettings` was falsy (no settings on the source shot), it would show a toast error "Source shot has no settings". Now `normalizeVideoTravelSettings` always returns a valid object (falling back to defaults if input is null/undefined), so it will silently apply default settings instead of showing an error. This is arguably correct -- applying defaults is reasonable -- but it's a subtle behavior change.

Here is my detailed assessment.

> AGENT

The `params` field on `GenerationRow` is `PersistedGenerationParams | undefined`. The `RawGenerationDbRow.params` is `Record<string, unknown> | null`. The old code was `gen.params as GenerationRow['params']` -- an unsafe cast. The new code `gen.params ?? undefined` converts `null` to `undefined` but still passes `Record<string, unknown>` where `PersistedGenerationParams` is expected. This is actually the same level of type safety (both rely on structural compatibility), but more explicit about the null-to-undefined conversion. Fine.

**FLAG -- Indentation bug.** Lines 72-79 have broken indentation. `initialClips.push({` is at 6 spaces (same level as `if`), but the properties inside the object literal are inconsistently indented -- `url`, `posterUrl`, `finalFrameUrl`, `durationSeconds` at 10 spaces, `loaded`, `playing` at 8 spaces, and the opening `initialClips.push` at 6. This is inside the `if (clip.url)` block so it should be at 8+ spaces. The code will still work since JS doesn't care about whitespace, but it's messy and suggests a bad merge/edit.

#### 3. `/src/tools/training-data-helper/hooks/useTrainingData.ts` -- CORRECT

Adds `throw error` after `normalizeAndPresentError` in three catch blocks (`updateSegment`, `deleteSegment`, `deleteVideo`). This is the correct pattern: present the error to the user, then re-throw so the caller can handle it (e.g., React Query can mark the mutation as failed). Previously, errors were silently swallowed after the toast.

#### 4. `/src/tools/training-data-helper/hooks/useTrainingDataBatches.ts` -- CORRECT

Same pattern: adds `throw error` after `normalizeAndPresentError` in `deleteBatch`.

#### 5. Test files for training-data-helper -- CORRECT

`useTrainingData.test.ts`: Adds mock for `normalizeAndPresentError` and a test that verifies `updateSegment` rethrows after presenting the error. The test correctly asserts both the throw and the mock call.

`useTrainingDataBatches.test.ts`: Same pattern for `deleteBatch`. Both tests are well-structured.

#### 6. `/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/useShotEditorController.ts` -- MIXED

**The big refactor:** Extracts the massive `useShotEditorController` function body into three helper hooks:
- `useShotEditorBootstrap` -- all hook calls and initialization
- `usePersistedShotEditorSettings` -- tool settings and generations pane settings
- `useShotEditorScreenAssembly` -- screen model + layout

**Positive:** This is a legitimate decomposition of a very large function. The type `ShotEditorBootstrapResult` makes the contract explicit.

**Issues found:**

**(a) `useEffect` dependency widened -- minor performance concern** (`/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/useShotEditorController.ts`, line 425)
```ts
useEffect(() => {
    if (simpleFilteredImages.length > 2 && turboMode) {
      motionSettings.setTurboMode(false);
    }
  }, [motionSettings, simpleFilteredImages.length, turboMode]);
```
Previously depended on `setTurboMode` (a stable function reference). Now depends on `motionSettings` (the whole memoized object), which re-creates whenever *any* motion setting changes (its `useMemo` depends on `handlers` which changes on every settings update). The effect body is guarded so it won't cause incorrect behavior, but it will fire unnecessarily.

**(b) Unused import removed: `type RefObject`** -- correct, no longer used.

**(c) Unused import removed: `type LoraModel`** in `useApplySettingsHandler.ts` -- correct.

**(d) Renamed variables:** `loraSettingsFromContext` -> `loraSettings`, `steerableMotionSettingsFromContext` -> `steerableMotionSettings`. Consistent rename, all references updated. Correct.

**(e) `ShotEditorBootstrapResult` type is verbose** -- every field uses `ReturnType<typeof useX>['fieldName']`, which is correct but makes the interface ~40 lines long. This is a style choice, not a bug.

#### 7. `/src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useShotSettings.ts` -- MOSTLY CORRECT

**Changes:**
- Replaces `videoTravelSettings.defaults` with `createDefaultVideoTravelSettings()` (returns a fresh clone each time, preventing shared-mutable-state bugs). Correct.
- Uses `normalizeVideoTravelSettings()` to validate/coerce persisted data before applying.
- Adds defensive spread for `steerableMotionSettings` with explicit `typeof` check.

**Behavior change in `applyShotSettings`** (line 190-194): Previously showed `toast.error('Source shot has no settings')` when source shot had no travel settings. Now `normalizeVideoTravelSettings(undefined)` returns defaults, which get silently applied. This means applying settings from a shot that never had settings configured will reset the target to defaults rather than showing an error. This could be confusing to users -- they click "copy settings from shot X" and their settings get wiped to defaults with no feedback. **This is a minor regression in UX.**

#### 8. `/src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/workflow/useVideoTravelData.ts` -- CORRECT

Wraps `toolSettingsQuery.settings` and `projectSettingsQuery.settings` through `normalizeVideoTravelSettings()`. The null-check guard (`? normalizeVideoTravelSettings(...) : undefined`) preserves the loading-state semantics.

#### 9. `/src/tools/travel-between-images/settings.ts` -- CORRECT, well-tested

Adds ~180 lines of normalization infrastructure:
- Helper functions (`asRecord`, `asString`, `asBoolean`, `asFiniteNumber`, `asEnum`)
- `normalizeVideoTravelSettings()` -- comprehensive field-by-field validation/coercion
- `normalizeShotLoras()`, `normalizePairConfigs()`, `normalizeShotImageIds()`, `normalizeStructureVideo()`
- `createDefaultVideoTravelSettings()` -- returns a fresh clone

**Quality:** This is solid defensive programming. Each normalizer validates types before use, filters invalid entries from arrays, and falls back to defaults. The `normalizeStructureVideo` correctly delegates to `migrateLegacyStructureVideos` when the canonical shape isn't found.

**Note on `asRecord` duplication**: The same `asRecord` helper appears in both `settings.ts` and `legacyStructureVideo.ts`. Not a bug, but worth noting.

#### 10. `/src/tools/travel-between-images/settings.test.ts` -- CORRECT

Adds two well-structured tests:
- Verifies normalization of a complete settings object with mixed valid/invalid data
- Verifies fallback to clean defaults for non-object input
- Tests correctly assert filtering of invalid array entries (loras without required fields, pair configs without id, non-string image ids)

---

### `src/domains/generation/` domain

#### 11. `/src/domains/generation/hooks/useGenerationMutations.ts` -- CORRECT

**(a) `thumbUrl` -> `thumbnailUrl` rename:** Consistent rename in `updateGenerationLocation` and `useUpdateGenerationLocation`. The inner call to `updateGenerationLocationInProject` already used `thumbnailUrl`, so this eliminates a naming mismatch at the boundary.

**(b) `createGeneration` now uses `coerceGenerationRowDto`:** Instead of directly casting `data as GenerationRowDto & { params?: Json | null }`, it now goes through runtime validation via `coerceGenerationRowDto`. If the shape is invalid, it throws with a clear error message. This is strictly safer than the previous blind cast.

**(c) Import cleanup:** Removes direct `GenerationRowDto` type import since the coercion function handles typing internally. Correct.

#### 12. `/src/domains/generation/hooks/__tests__/useGenerationMutations.test.ts` -- CORRECT

Updates test description and variable name from `thumbUrl` to `thumbnailUrl`. Matches the implementation change.

#### 13. `/src/domains/generation/mappers/generationRowMapper.ts` -- CORRECT

Adds `coerceGenerationRowDto` (~60 lines) with helper functions. This function safely coerces an `unknown` value into a typed `GenerationRowDto` by:
- Verifying it's a non-null, non-array object with a string `id`
- Type-checking each optional field before including it
- Using conditional spread to only include fields that pass validation

**Quality:** The spread-heavy approach (`...(condition ? { field: value } : {})`) is verbose but correct. The `satisfies` assertion at the end ensures type safety without runtime cost.

---

### `src/domains/lora/` domain

#### 14. `/src/domains/lora/components/HuggingFaceTokenSetup.tsx` -- CORRECT

Wraps `saveToken` call in a try/catch. Previously, if `saveToken` threw (as opposed to returning `{ success: false }`), the error would be unhandled. Now both failure modes are covered:
- `result.success === false` -> show `result.error`
- Exception thrown -> show `error.message`

Both paths correctly set state to `'error'`. Good defensive fix.

---

### Summary of Flags

| Severity | File | Issue |
|----------|------|-------|
| **Medium** | `src/tools/join-clips/lib/clipBootstrap.ts:72-79` | Broken indentation in `initialClips.push` block. Code works but formatting is wrong. |
| **Low** | `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useShotSettings.ts:190-194` | `applyShotSettings` no longer shows error toast when source shot has no settings; silently applies defaults instead. Subtle UX regression. |
| **Low** | `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/useShotEditorController.ts:425` | `useEffect` dependency changed from stable `setTurboMode` to potentially-unstable `motionSettings` object. Won't cause bugs but may fire unnecessarily. |
| **Informational** | `settings.ts` + `legacyStructureVideo.ts` | `asRecord` helper function duplicated across files. |

Everything else looks correct: import migrations are consistent, error re-throwing is properly tested, the `coerceGenerationRowDto` runtime validation is a clear safety improvement, the `normalizeVideoTravelSettings` normalizer is thorough and well-tested, and the HuggingFace token error handling fix is sound.

This is a pre-existing issue (missing `toolSettingsService` module) unrelated to the current diff changes. The test logic itself is correct.

Here is my detailed assessment:

---

## Assessment of Uncommitted Changes in shared/hooks, shared/lib, and shared/components

### 1. `src/shared/lib/toolSettingsWriteService.ts` -- API Signature Change

**Change**: Replaced overloaded positional args (`signalOrMode?: AbortSignal | SettingsWriteMode, maybeMode?: SettingsWriteMode`) with a single options object (`options: UpdateToolSettingsOptions = {}`).

**Logic**: Correct. Cleaner API, no ambiguity.

**BUG FOUND -- Missed caller at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/settings/hooks/useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts` line 205:**
```typescript
updateToolSettingsSupabase({
  scope: flushScope,
  id: pendingForEntity,
  toolId: flushToolId,
  patch: pending,
}, 'immediate')  // <-- STILL USING OLD POSITIONAL API
```
This passes the string `'immediate'` as the `options` parameter. Since `isSettingsWriteMode(options.mode)` will evaluate `options.mode` on a string (which is `undefined`), it silently falls back to `'debounced'`. **This is a correctness regression**: settings flush on unmount/navigation will be debounced instead of immediate, potentially losing user changes.

**Fix**: Change to `{ mode: 'immediate' }`.

**Doc staleness**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/docs/structure_detail/settings_system.md` line 149 still shows the old 3-arg API: `await updateToolSettingsSupabase({ scope, id, toolId, patch }, undefined, 'immediate');`. Should be updated to `{ mode: 'immediate' }`.

**Tests**: Updated correctly in both `useToolSettings.test.ts` and `toolSettingsWriteService.test.ts`.

---

### 2. `src/shared/hooks/segments/segmentDataTransforms.ts` -- Runtime Validation

**Change**: Added `coerceRawGenerationDbRow` / `coerceRawGenerationDbRows` functions that validate raw DB data at runtime instead of using unsafe `as unknown as RawGenerationDbRow[]` casts.

**Logic**: Correct. Each field is validated with appropriate type guards (`asNullableString`, `asNullableNumber`, `asRecord`). The conditional spread pattern (`...(condition ? { field: value } : {})`) correctly preserves optional fields -- only including them when the source data has them.

**Minor concern**: Lines 32-42 are verbose with repeated `asNullableString(record.X) !== undefined` checks. This is functionally correct but could be DRYer. Not a bug, just stylistic.

**Behavior change in `transformToGenerationRow`**: `params: gen.params as GenerationRow['params']` changed to `params: gen.params ?? undefined`. This correctly converts `null` to `undefined` to match the `GenerationRow.params?: PersistedGenerationParams` type. Good fix.

**Integration in `segmentOutputsQueries.ts`**: Both `fetchParentGenerations` and `fetchChildGenerations` now route through `coerceRawGenerationDbRows` instead of raw casts. Correct.

**Tests**: New test case added for `coerceRawGenerationDbRows` validation. The test checks that a row missing `id` at index 1 throws `'Invalid generation row at index 1'`. Covers the error path. The happy path is implicitly covered by existing tests that call `transformToGenerationRow` on the output.

**Potential concern with `coerceRawGenerationDbRows` throwing**: If Supabase ever returns a malformed row, this will throw and reject the React Query promise, which should surface as an error state. This is acceptable -- failing loudly on corrupt data is better than silently processing garbage.

---

### 3. `src/shared/hooks/segments/useSegmentMutations.ts` -- Settings Write Mode

**Change**: Two calls updated from `'immediate'` positional arg to `{ mode: 'immediate' }` options object.

**Logic**: Correct, consistent with the API change.

---

### 4. `src/shared/hooks/projects/services/projectSetupRepository.ts` -- Error Cause Chain

**Change**: `throw new Error(message)` changed to `throw new Error(message, { cause: error })` in `throwProjectSetupRepositoryError`.

**Logic**: Correct. Preserves the original error for debugging while keeping the same user-facing message.

**Tests**: Updated to assert `toMatchObject({ message, cause: rawError })` instead of just `toThrow('delete failed')`. The test now also specifies a realistic `code: '23503'` on the raw error. Good.

---

### 5. `src/shared/lib/settingsMigration.ts` -- Phase Config Decoding + LoRA Hardening

**Changes**:
- `migrateLoraConfig` now returns `null` for invalid entries (missing id/name/path) instead of returning objects with empty strings. Uses `toString()` and `toNumber()` helpers instead of unsafe casts.
- `migrateLoras` filters out null entries.
- New `decodePhaseConfig` / `decodePhaseSettings` / `decodePhaseLoraConfig` functions replace unsafe `toRecord(x) as unknown as SegmentSettings['phaseConfig']` casts.
- `loras` field in `readShotSettings` now pre-filters with `toRecord` before passing to `migrateLoras`.
- New import of `PhaseConfig`, `PhaseLoraConfig`, `PhaseSettings` types from `@/shared/types/phaseConfig`.

**Logic**: All correct. The `decodePhaseConfig` function validates all required fields and returns `undefined` if any are missing. The `mode` field is correctly optional with `'i2v' | 'vace'` validation. The `stepsPerPhase` uses `toNumberArray` which validates every element.

**One subtlety**: `decodePhaseConfig` returns `undefined` (not the input) if validation fails. In `readShotSettings`, `phaseConfig: decodePhaseConfig(raw.phaseConfig)` means invalid phase configs will silently become `undefined`, losing the user's (corrupted) data. This is the correct behavior -- better to fall back to defaults than to pass garbage to the video generation worker.

**No test updates for settingsMigration**: The phase config decoding and LoRA hardening have no dedicated test coverage in this diff. There are no existing `settingsMigration.test.ts` tests visible. Not blocking, but worth noting.

---

### 6. `src/shared/lib/joinClips/defaults.ts` -- Legacy Field Removal

**Change**: Removed `startingVideoUrl`, `startingVideoPosterUrl`, `endingVideoUrl`, `endingVideoPosterUrl` from defaults. Updated comment from "New multi-clip format" to "Canonical multi-clip format".

**Logic**: Correct cleanup. These were explicitly `undefined` placeholder fields.

**Tests**: The test that asserted these fields were `undefined` was removed. The remaining tests cover `clips: []` and other defaults. Correct.

---

### 7. `src/shared/components/MediaSelectionPanel.tsx` -- Import Path Change

**Change**: `import { MediaGallery, ... } from '@/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery'` changed to `from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery'`.

**Logic**: Correct. Verified that `DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS` and `GalleryFilterState` are both exported from `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.ts`.

---

### 8. `src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem/hooks/useMediaGalleryItemShotWorkflow.ts` and `useMediaGalleryItemTaskShare.ts` -- Import Path Changes

**Change**: Both files change imports of `GeneratedImageWithMetadata` and `SimplifiedShotOption` from `@/features/gallery/components/MediaGallery/types` to `@/shared/components/MediaGallery/types`.

**Logic**: Correct. Both types are confirmed exported from the new path.

---

### 9. `src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem.test.tsx` -- Deleted

**Change**: 319-line test file deleted.

**Concern**: This was the only integration test for the `MediaGalleryItem` component itself. Sub-component/hook tests exist (`ItemMetadataBar.test.tsx`, `ItemShotBadges.test.tsx`, `useImageLoading.test.ts`, etc.) but they don't cover the top-level rendering logic (placeholder skeleton branch, draggable branch, prefetch-on-hover). This is test coverage loss.

```
 | `/src/pages` | Top-level pages | `HomePage`, `ShotsPage`, `ArtPage`, `SharePage`, `NotFoundPage`, payment pages |

+| `/src/features` | Feature slices (tasks, shots, gallery, resources, projects, settings, etc.) | UI + hooks organized by product feature |
 | `/src/integrations` | Third-party integrations | Supabase client, auth, realtime, instrumentation |
 | `/src/types` | Shared TypeScript types | `database.ts`, `tasks.ts`, `ai.ts`, `env.ts` |
 | `/src/shared` | Cross-domain primitives and neutral contracts | `components/ui/` (presentational primitives only), reusable contracts/types, shared infra that does not own product workflows |
@@ -92,7 +92,7 @@ Tools live in `/src/tools/{tool-name}/` following a consistent structure. See [a
 - Keep product workflows, Supabase-backed repositories, and stateful feature orchestration in `/src/domains`, `/src/features`, or the owning `/src/tools/*` module.
 - `src/shared/components/ui/` is reserved for low-level presentational primitives and wrappers; feature-aware controls such as AI prompt actions belong in a feature/shared-product folder, not the UI primitive root.
 - Cross-surface contracts should live in neutral shared type files instead of importing from a concrete widget folder just to reach a type.
-- Feature-owned entrypoints such as `features/resources/*` or `features/shots/*` should host the real implementation. If an old `shared/*` path must stay alive for compatibility, keep it as a thin shim with an explicit comment instead of a second implementation home.
+- Feature-owned entrypoints such as `features/resources/*`, `features/projects/services/*`, or `features/shots/*` should host the real implementation. Domain-backed helpers like generation navigation should live with the owning domain instead of under `shared/*`. If an old `shared/*` path must stay alive for compatibility, keep it as a thin shim with an explicit comment instead of a second implementation home.
 
 ### Settings Resolution
 Priority: **shot → project → user → defaults**. See [settings_system.md](docs/structure_detail/settings_system.md).
```

```
    10→
    11→export async function cleanupFailedProjectSetup(projectId: string, userId: string): Promise<void> {
    12→  try {
    13→    await deleteProjectForUser(projectId, userId);
    14→  } catch (error) {
    15→    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
    16→      context: 'projectSetupService.cleanupFailedProjectSetup',
    17→      showToast: false,
    18→    });
    19→  }
    20→}
    21→
    22→export async function createDefaultShotForProject(
    23→  projectId: string,
    24→  options?: {
    25→    initialSettings?: Record<string, Json | undefined>;
    26→    isFirstProject?: boolean;
    27→  },
    28→): Promise<string> {
    29→  const isFirstProject = options?.isFirstProject === true;
    30→  const shotName = isFirstProject ? 'Getting Started' : 'Default Shot';
    31→
    32→  const shot = await createDefaultShotRecord(
    33→    projectId,
    34→    shotName,
    35→    options?.initialSettings || {},
    36→  );
    37→
    38→  if (isFirstProject) {
    39→    await copyOnboardingTemplateToProject(projectId, shot.id);
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  return shot.id;
    43→}
    44→
    45→export async function ensureUserRecordExists(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    46→  const existingUser = await hasUserRecord(userId);
    47→
    48→  if (!existingUser) {
    49→    try {
    50→      await createUserRecordIfMissing();
    51→    } catch (userError) {
    52→      normalizeAndPresentError(userError, {
    53→        context: 'projectSetupService.ensureUserRecordExists',
    54→        showToast: false,
    55→      });
    56→    }
    57→  }
    58→}
    59→
    60→export async function createDefaultShotWithRollback(
    61→  projectId: string,
    62→  userId: string,
    63→  options?: {
    64→    initialSettings?: Record<string, Json | undefined>;
    65→    isFirstProject?: boolean;
    66→  },
    67→): Promise<void> {

    70→  } catch (setupError) {
    71→    await cleanupFailedProjectSetup(projectId, userId);
```

```
1→import { getSupabaseClient } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     2→import type { Json } from '@/integrations/supabase/jsonTypes';
     3→
     4→function throwProjectSetupRepositoryError(error: unknown, fallbackMessage: string): never {
     5→  if (error instanceof Error) {
     6→    throw error;
     7→  }
     8→  if (
     9→    error
    10→    && typeof error === 'object'
    11→    && 'message' in error
    12→    && typeof (error as { message?: unknown }).message === 'string'
    13→  ) {
    14→    throw new Error((error as { message: string }).message, { cause: error });
    15→  }
    16→
    17→  throw new Error(fallbackMessage, { cause: error });
    18→}
    19→
    20→export async function copyOnboardingTemplateToProject(
    21→  targetProjectId: string,
    22→  targetShotId: string,
    23→): Promise<void> {
    24→  const { error } = await getSupabaseClient().rpc('copy_onboarding_template', {
    25→    target_project_id: targetProjectId,
    26→    target_shot_id: targetShotId,
    27→  });
    28→
    29→  if (error) {
    30→    throwProjectSetupRepositoryError(error, 'Failed to copy onboarding template');
    31→  }
    32→}
    33→
    34→export async function deleteProjectForUser(projectId: string, userId: string): Promise<void> {
    35→  const { error } = await getSupabaseClient().from('projects')
    36→    .delete()
    37→    .eq('id', projectId)
    38→    .eq('user_id', userId);
    39→
    40→  if (error) {
    41→    throwProjectSetupRepositoryError(error, 'Failed to delete project');
    42→  }
    43→}
    44→
    45→export async function createDefaultShotRecord(
    46→  projectId: string,
    47→  name: string,
    48→  settings: Record<string, Json | undefined>,
    49→): Promise<{ id: string }> {
    50→  const { data, error } = await getSupabaseClient().from('shots')
    51→    .insert({
    52→      name,
    53→      project_id: projectId,
    54→      settings,
    55→    })
    56→    .select('id')
    57→    .single();
    58→
    59→  if (error) {
    60→    throwProjectSetupRepositoryError(error, 'Failed to create default shot');
    61→  }
    62→  if (!data?.id) {
    63→    throw new Error('Default shot creation returned no shot id');
    64→  }
    65→
    66→  return { id: data.id };
    67→}
    68→
    69→export async function hasUserRecord(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    70→  const { data, error } = await getSupabaseClient().from('users')
    71→    .select('id')
    72→    .eq('id', userId)
    73→    .maybeSingle();
    74→
    75→  if (error) {
    76→    throwProjectSetupRepositoryError(error, 'Failed to check user record');
    77→  }
    78→
    79→  return Boolean(data?.id);
    80→}
    81→
    82→export async function createUserRecordIfMissing(): Promise<void> {
    83→  const { error } = await getSupabaseClient().rpc('create_user_record_if_not_exists');
    84→
    85→  if (error) {
    86→    throwProjectSetupRepositoryError(error, 'Failed to create user record');
    87→  }
    88→}
    89→
```
